(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟip peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum - oṟṟāy niṉṟa nakarattiṉ mēlāya nakarattoṭu moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nunti eṉa varum.
ivvāṟu mutalākkam kūṟavē, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṭam nunti eṉṉum muṟippeyar. paṟṟukkōṭu nakaramici nakaram.	(34)
atu muṟṟukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟupaṭāmi
68.	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
	appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiya lāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu - (am mutaṟkaṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram āṇṭu itaḻkuvittuk kūṟum vaḻi) muṟṟukarat toṭu piṟa ukarampōlap poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu; (yāṇṭeṉiṉ,) ap peyar maruṅkiṉ nili iyalāṉa - am muṟippeyariṭattut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāku eṉavum, naku eṉavum kuṟukiyum, kuṟukātum niṉṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla, nunti eṉṟu kuṟukkamāya vaḻiyum, itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟak kuṟukātavaḻiyum, poruḷ vēṟupaṭāta vāṟu aṟika.
iṉi 'iraṭṭuṟamoḻital' eṉpataṉāṉ, poruḷ eṉṟataṉi iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum ākki, piṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla īṇṭi ukaram iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum vēṟupaṭātu eṉpatu koḷka.	(35)
aukāram nīṅkalāṉa uyirkaḷ moḻikku īṟātal
69.	uyiru eñciya iṟuti yākum.
iḵtu, uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir au eñciya iṟuti ākum - uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintavi moḻikku īṟām, aukārantāṉ īṟākātu e-ṟu.
iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.
e-ṭu:	āa, ā, īi, ī, ūu, ū, ēe, ē, aii, ai, ōo, ō eṉa uyir īṟāyiṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttintum aḷapeṭi vakiyāṉ īṟāyiṉa.
uyirmeykaḷum, mēlvariyaṟi kūṟātaṉavākiya akara ākāra ikara īkāra aikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyintaṉa īṇṭē koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viḷa, palā,kiḷi, kurī, paṉi eṉa varum.	(36)
aukāram kavavōṭu iyintu īṟātal
70.	kavavō ṭiyiyiṉ auvu mākum.
iḵtu, īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻi ām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kava ōṭu iyiyiṉ - kakara vakaramākiya meykaḷoṭu poruntiṉ, auvum ākum - muṉ īṟākā teṉṟa aukāramum īṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: ku, vu eṉa varum.	(37)
ekaram meyyōṭiyintu īṟākāmi
71.	eeṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
iḵtu, ekaram tāṉē niṉṟavaḻiyaṉṟi meyyoṭukūṭi īṟākā teṉa variyaṟi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e eṉa varum uyir - e eṉṟu colla varum uyir, mey īṟu ākātu - tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meyyōṭu iyintu īṟākātu eṅiṟu.	(38)
okaram nakaramey allavaṟṟōṭu iyintu īṟākāmi
72.	ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) ovvum aṟṟu - okaramum tāṉē niṉṟu īṟāva taṉṟi meyyōṭu iyintu īṟākātu, na alaṅkaṭi - nakara meyyō ṭallāta viṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nu eṉa varum.	(39)
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ñakarattōṭu iyintu īṟākāmi
73.	ēō eṉumuyir ñakārat tilli.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattu illi - ē ō eṉṟu collappaṭum uyir (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum īṟāvataṉṟi) ñakarattōṭu īṟāvatilli e-ṟu.
tāmē yātal muṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa voḻiya iṟavātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(40)
ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ nakaravakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyintu īṟākāmi
74.	uū kāram navavoṭu navilā.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) u ūkāram na va oṭu navilā - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilvataṉṟi) nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu payilā e-ṟu.
tāmē īṟātal mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa vallātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'navilā' eṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmi novvu kavvu eṉṟu (ukaram) vakārattōṭu īṟātal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmi nakarattōṭu varavu uṇṭēṉum koḷka.	(41)
uccakāram irumoḻikkē urittātal
75.	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē.
ituvum atu orōvaḻi variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāram irumoḻikku urittu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakaram *irumoḻikku īṟākum; palamoḻikku īṟākātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ucu, mucu eṉa varum. pacuveṉpatu āriyac citivu.	(42)
*(pāṭam) 'irumoḻikku allātu' eṉpatu.
uppakāram orumoḻikkē urittātalum, ataṉporuḷ vēṟupāṭum
76.	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟiyum moḻiyatu poruḷ vēṟupāṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāram oṉṟu eṉa moḻipa - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram oru moḻikku īṟām eṉṟu colluvar, iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭu ākum - atutāṉ taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭat tum nilipeṟum poruṇmittām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tapu eṉa varum. itu paṭuttuc colla, nī cā eṉat taṉ viṉiyām. eṭuttuc colla, nī oṉṟaṉic cāvippi eṉap piṟa viṉiyām.	(43)
moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātal
77.	eñciya vellām eñcutal ilavē.
itu, moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciya ellām eñcutal ila - moḻikku īṟākātu niṉṟa uyirmeykaḷellām tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻipu ila e-ṟu.
eñciya uyirmeyyāvaṉa: aukāram kakāra vakāraṅkaḷi oḻinta meyyōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum, ekaram ellā meyyōṭum iyinta uyirmeyyum, okaram nakaram oḻinta meyyōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum, ēkāra ōkāram ñakārattōṭu iyinta uyir meyyum, u ūkāram nakara vakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum eṉa ivi.
e-ṭu:	tam peyarkku īṟu āmāṟu: ṅuk kaḷintār eṉavum, kek kaḷintār eṉavum, kok kaḷintār eṉavum, ñēk kaḷintār eṉavum, ñōk kaḷintār eṉavum, nuk kaḷintār eṉavum, nūk kaḷintār eṉavum, vuk kaḷintār eṉavum, vūk kaḷintār eṉavum varum.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, moḻikku īṟāy niṉṟa uyir meykaḷum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum īṟām eṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kak kaḷintār, kā valitu eṉavum, ak kaḷintār, ā valitu eṉavum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka.
taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi, moḻikaṭku itu karuviyāka īṟṟakattu muṭipu 'oṉṟiṉa muṭittalāṟ' koḷka.	(44)
moḻikku īṟām taṉimeykaḷ
78.	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu, taṉimeykaḷuḷ moḻikku īṟāvaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum ap patiṉoṉṟē - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ap patiṉoṉṟumē, puḷḷi iṟuti - puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉa varum.
ṉakāratti īṟṟu viyātu, makarattoṭu vittatu ataṉ mayakka iyipu nōkki eṉṟuṇarka.	(45)
nakaramey irumoḻikkē īṟātal
79.	ucca kāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakārattōṭē nakāram porunti aḵtu irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōlat tāṉum irumoḻikku īṟām e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	porun, verin eṉa varum.	(46)
ñakaramey orumoḻikkē īṟātal
80.	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟē
	apporuḷ iraṭṭā tivaṇi yāṉa.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakarattoṭu ñakāramum attaṉmittāy orumoḻikku īṟām, ivaṇiyāṉa a poruḷ iraṭṭātu - ivviṭattu ataṉ poruḷ av vuppakāram pōla iruporuḷ paṭātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ eṉa varum.
ñakāram orumoḻikku īṟātaliṉ, nakarattiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu.(47)
vakaramey īṟām coṟkaḷ nāṉkē ātal
81.	vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu - vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉkumoḻi īṟṟatām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	av, iv, uv, tev eṉa varum.	(48)
makara īṟṟoṭu mayaṅkāta ṉakara īṟṟuttoṭar moḻikaḷ
oṉpatu eṉal
82.	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa.
ituvum orōvaḻi moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) pukar aṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa - kuṟṟam aṟac collappaṭṭa aḵṟiṇiyiṭattu, makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta - makaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyoṭu mayaṅkāteṉṟu variyaṟukkappaṭṭa, ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - ṉakaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilaṉ nilam, pilaṉ pilam eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mayaṅkuvaṉa. iṉi mayaṅkātaṉa: ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kuyiṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ tiripuṭiyaṉa kaḷika. oṉpaḵtu eṉṉum āytam ceyyuḷ vikāram. aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟatu īṇṭu aḵṟiṇip peyariṉi.	(49)
moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

3
piṟappiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ  piṟappiyal  eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉi nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉē vikkaveṉiṉ, cārpil tōṟṟat teḻuttum taṉimeyyum moḻimarapiṭi uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻi marapiṉ piṉṉatāyiṟṟu.
eḻuttukkaḷatu potuppiṟappu : iṭamum muyaṟciyum
83.	unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
	taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum niliip
	pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
	aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
	uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
	ellā veḻuttuñ colluṅ kālip
	piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
	tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu potup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum neṟippaṭa nāṭi collum kāli - tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷellām oruvaṉ muṟippaṭa ārāyntu tammic colluṅkālattu, unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi - koppūḻ aṭiyāka mēlē kiḷarkiṉṟa utāṉaṉ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiya vāyut tōṉṟi, taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilii - taliyiṉ kaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nili peṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ uṟuppu uṟṟu amiya - (taliyum miṭaṟum neñcum eṉṉum mūṉṟa ṉōṭum) pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṭṭākiya muṟimiyiyuṭiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ ōr uṟuppōṭu ōr uṟupput tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa, piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala - piṟappiṉatu ākkam vēṟu vēṟu pulappaṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaluṭiya, tiṟappaṭat teriyum kāṭciyāṉa - kūṟupāṭuḷatāka ārāyum aṟiviṭattu e-ṟu.
itaḻpōṟalāṉ vāy itaḻeṉappaṭṭatu. 'ellā eḻuttum' eṉṉum eḻuvāykkup 'piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvēṟu iyala' eṉpataṉi orucoṉ ṉīrmippaṭuttip payaṉiliyākkuka.	(1)
uyireḻuttu miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ icittal
84.	avvaḻip
	paṉṉī ruyirum tannili tiriyā
	miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icikkum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttiṟkup potuppiṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vaḻi - avviṭattu, paṉṉīr uyirum tam nili tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum tattam niliyiṉ tiriyāvāy, miṭaṟṟu piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṭ piṟanta vaḷiyāṉ olikkum e-ṟu.
'tannili tiriyā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram tannili tiriyumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
uyireḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal
85.	avaṟṟuḷ
	aā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttukkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyir paṉṉiraṇṭa ṉuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu aṅkāntu iyalum - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya av viraṇṭum aṅkāntu colla aḵtu iṭamākap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(3)
86.	iī eē aiyeṉa icikkum
	appāl aintum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	avitām
	aṇpal mutalnā viḷimpuṟal uṭiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) i ī e ē ai eṉa icikkum a pāl aintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu collappaṭum ak kūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - mēṟkūṟiya akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu collap piṟakkum, avitām palaṇ nāmutal viḷimpu uṟal uṭiya - avitām (avvāṟu collap piṟakkumiṭattup) palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉi nāviṉatu aṭiyiṉ viḷimpu ceṉṟu uṟutali yuṭiya e-ṟu.	(4)
87.	uū oō aueṉa icikkum
	appāl aintum itaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō au eṉa icikkum a pāl aintum - u ū o ō au eṉac colla icikkum ak kūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuc colla naṭakkum e-ṟu.	(5)
uyir, mey ivaṟṟiṉ piṟavikkup puṟaṉaṭi
88.	tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.
itu, muṉkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum oru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tamtam tiripu ciṟiya eṉpa - (eḻuttukkaḷ oru tāṉattuk kūṭip piṟakku meṉappaṭṭaṉa; avvāṟu kūṭip piṟappiṉum,) tattam vēṟupāṭukaḷic ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷeṉṟu colluvar. av vēṟupāṭu aṟintukoḷka e-ṟu.	(6)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ka, ṅa - meykaḷ
89.	kakāra ṅakāram mutalnā aṇṇam.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kakāram ṅakāram nāmutal aṇṇam (mutal) - kakāramum ṅakāramum nāmutalum aṇṇamutalum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu. 	(7)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ca, ña - meykaḷ
90.	cakāra ñakāram iṭinā aṇṇam.
(i-ḷ.) cakāram ñakāram nāiṭi aṇṇam (iṭi) - cakāramum ñakāramum nāvatu iṭiyum aṇṇattatu iṭiyum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(8)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ṭa, ṇa - meykaḷ
91.	ṭakāra ṇakāram nuṉinā aṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṭakāram ṇakāram nānuṉi aṇṇam (nuṉi) - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nāvatu nuṉiyum aṇṇattatu nuṉiyum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(9)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - mēlaṉa āṟum mūvakip piṟappiṉa ātal
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a āṟu eḻuttum mūvaki piṟappiṉa - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa āṟu eḻuttum niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉ (aṟuvakip piṟappiṉa alla) mūvakip piṟappiṉa e-ṟu.	(10)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ta, na - meykaḷ
93.	aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkiṉ
	nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa voṟṟat
	tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
ituvum meykaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam naṇṇiya pal mutal maruṅkiṉ - aṇṇattip poruntiya palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē, nānuṉi parantu mey uṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu vaṭivi uṟumpaṭi oṟṟa, tām iṉitu piṟakkum - tām iṉitākap piṟakkum, takāram nakāram - takāramum nakāramum e-ṟu.
muṉṉē 'uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya' (83) eṉṟu vittup piṉṉum 'meyyuṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellā eḻuttukkaḷum meyyuṟṟapōtē iṉitu piṟappaveṉpatu koḷka.	(11)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ṟa, ṉa - meykaḷ
94.	aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa
	ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa - aṇarntu nuṉinā aṇṇattic ceṉṟu oṟṟa, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya av viraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
iṅkuniṉṟum neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟimi viṭṭu nā atikāram paṭṭatu kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟateṉa vuṇarka.	(12)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ra, ḻa - meykaḷ
95.	nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa
	rakāra ḻakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa - nuṉinā aṇarntu aṇṇattit taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(13)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - la, ḷa - meykaḷ
96.	nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpal mutaluṟa
	āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟavum varuṭavum
	lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nā viḷimpu vīṅki pal aṇ mutal uṟa - nāviṉatu viḷimpu taṭittup palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattip porunta, a vayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramum varuṭa ḷakāramum a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - avviṭattu (mutalnā) av vaṇṇatti oṟṟa lakāramum ataṉit taṭava ḷakāramumāka av viraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(14)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - pa, ma - meykaḷ
97.	itaḻiyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) itaḻ iyintu piṟakkum pakāram makāram - kīḻ itaḻum mēl itaḻum tammil iyiyap piṟakkum pakāramum makāramum e-ṟu.	(15)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - vakāramey
98.	pallitaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) pal itaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpallum kīḻ itaḻum tammil iyiya vakāram piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(16)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - yakāramey
99.	aṇṇañ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyici
	kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭiya yakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam cērnta miṭaṟṟu eḻu vaḷiici kaṇ uṟṟu aṭiya yakāram piṟakkum - aṇṇatti nāc cērnta viṭattu miṭaṟṟiṉiṉṟum eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya ici av vaṇṇatti aṇintu ceṟiya yakāram piṟakkum. 	(17)
melleḻuttukkaḷ mūkkuvaḷiyiciyāṉ tōṉṟutal
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappiṉ ākkañ
	colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum
	mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu, melleḻuttiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkam mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattē nili peṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷi ici yāppuṟa tōṉṟum - avi mūkkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vaḷiyatu iciyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
'yāppuṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṭiyeḻuttiṟku miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum, valleḻuttiṟkut talivaḷiyum koḷka.	(18)
cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu
101.	cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
	tērntuveḷip paṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum
	tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
	otta kāṭciyiṉ tammiyal piyalum.
itu, cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cārntuvariṉ allatu tamakku iyalpu ila eṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum - cilavaṟṟic cārntu variṉ allatu tamakkut tāmē varum iyalpu ilaveṉṟu ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa oḻinta mūṉṟum, tamtam cārpiṉ piṟappoṭu civaṇi otta kāṭci yiṉ tam iyalpu iyalum - tattamakkuc cārpākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟap piṭattē piṟattaloṭu porunti avviṭattē tamakkuriya iyalpiṉ naṭakkum e-ṟu.
'otta kāṭci' eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytattiṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttuc cārpēyeṉiṉum talivaḷiyāṟ piṟattaliṉ, uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttuc cārpākavē piṟakkumeṉpatu koḷka. 'tammiyal piyalum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭiyum uyirmeyyum tamakku aṭiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉpatu koḷka.	(19)
uḷniṉṟeḻum vaḷiyici yārāycci maṟiyiṉkaṇṇatu eṉal
102.	ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
	colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ
	piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi uṟaḻcci vārat(tu)
	akatteḻu vaḷiyici yariltapa nāṭi
	aḷapiṟ kōṭal antaṇar maṟittē.
iḵtu, ellā eḻuttiṟkum āvatoru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum - ellā eḻuttukkaḷum, veḷippaṭa kiḷantu colliya paḷḷi - veḷippaṭa vitantu collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ - eḻukiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉē, piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - tām piṟakkun toḻiluṭiyavātaloṭu tammic collum iṭattu, uṟaḻcci vārattu akattu eḻuvaḷi ici - tiritaruṅ kūṟṟiyuṭiya uḷniṉṟu eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya iciyi, aril tapa nāṭi - piṇakkamaṟa ārāyntu, aḷapiṉ kōṭal - māttiri variyaṟiyāṟ kōṭal, antaṇar maṟittu - pārppār vētattuk kaṇṇatu e-ṟu.
'uṟaḻcci vāram' eṉṟatu, untimutalā eḻum vaḷi talikāṟum ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟutali eṉak koḷka. 'vaḷi' eṉṉātu 'vaḷiyici' eṉṟatu, avvāṟu neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟum aḷavē vaḷi eṉappaṭum; piṉṉi neñciṉiṉṟum eḻuvuḻiyellām vaḷittaṉmi *tirintu eḻuttām taṉmiyatām eṉpatu viḷakki niṉṟatu.	(20)
*(pāṭam) 'cārntu' eṉpatu.
mēlic cūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi 
(puṟatticikkum oli poruḷpaṭuvataṟkē māttiri kūṟappaṭum eṉṟal)
103.	aḵtivaṇ nuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikkum
	meyteri vaḷiyici aḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - ('akatteḻu vaḷiyici aḷapiṟ kōṭalākiya') ataṉi in nūliṭattuc collātu, eḻuntu puṟattu icikkum - (akattiṉiṉṟu) eḻuntu puṟattup pōntu icikkum, mey teri vaḷi ici aḷapu - poruṇmi terikiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉāya iciyatu māttiriyiṉi, nuvaṉṟiciṉ - yāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉēṉ e-ṟu.
maṟṟu, iḵtu "aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittu" (piṟappiyal 20) eṉavē peṟappaṭṭataṉṟōveṉiṉ, 'antaṇar maṟittu' eṉpatu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟutaṟkum, piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟṟu nērntu uṭampaṭutaṟkum oppak kiṭantamiyiṉ av viyam tīrtaṟkuk kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. puṟattu icippataṉ muṉṉar, akattu icikkum vaḷi yiciyi am maṟikkaṇ ōr eḻuttiṟku mūṉṟu nili uḷatākak kūṟum (ataṉ āciriyaṉ); aḵtu āmāṟu aṟintu koḷka.
'meyteri' eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṟku mutaliyaṉa muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkumeṉiṉum, poruḷ teriyā nilimiya vākaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku aḷapu kūṟārāyiṉāreṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
'nuvaṉṟu' eṉpatu īṇṭu iṟantakālat taṉmi viṉi.	(21)

piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

4
puṇariyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷ, (mēṟ ceyki yōttukkaḷuḷ) puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpu kūṟiṉami yiṉ puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻimarapiṉōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
ellāc coṟkaḷiṉ īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirumāy aṭaṅkutal
104.	mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	iraṇṭutali yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵtu
	aṟunāṉ kīṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
	ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum
	meyyē yuyireṉṟu āyī riyala.
it talic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutalum īṟum kūṟiyavaḻi uyirum meyyum uyir meyyum eṉa mūṉṟāy virintu niṉṟataṉi uyirum meyyum eṉa iraṇṭākat tokuttalāṉum, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉak kūṟaliṉ, muppattu mūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉapōl virintataṉi aviyellām muppattu mūṉṟaṉuḷḷaṉavē eṉat tokuttalāṉum virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttiṉ - mūṉṟaṉi muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttiṉuḷ, iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa mutal āku irupaḵtu - av viraṇṭaṉi muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa moḻikku mutalāya irupatum, aṟunāṉku īṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟu iyalum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟōṭu vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ niṉṟu naṭakkum, ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum - mūvaki moḻikkum īṟum mutalu māvaṉa, mey uyir eṉṟu a īr iyala - meyyum uyirumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉi yuṭiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maram, ili, āl, viḷa eṉa meyyum uyirum mutalum īṟum āyiṉa.
irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu mutalāvaṉa  paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivi. irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu īṟāvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivi.
'īṟṟoṭu' eṉpatu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. mey mutaṟ kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, nālvakip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāveṉpatu koḷka.	(1)
moḻiyīṟākiya mey puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟṟal
105.	avaṟṟuḷ
	meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ taṉimeyyum mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉi vilakkaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēl meyyum uyirum eṉṟu kūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ, īṟu meyyellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal - moḻikku īṟāya meyyellām puḷḷi peṟutaloṭu niṟkum; mutalāyavi yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maram eṉa varum.
'moḻimutaṉ mey puḷḷiyoṭu nillātu' eṉṉātu, 'īṟel lām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal' eṉa īṟṟiṉmēl vittuk kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, av vīṟṟiṉ mey uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu aḵtu ēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
kuṟṟiyalukarīṟum puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭamkoṭuttal
106.	kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku oru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu eṉa moḻipa - īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum (puḷḷi yīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) at taṉmittu eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭic cēṟal eṉa vuṇarka.	(3)
uyirmeyyīṟu uyirīṟākak koḷḷappaṭutal
107.	uyirmey yīṟum uyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēl "meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala" (puṇariyal 1) eṉṟataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey īṟum uyir īṟṟu iyaṟṟu - uyirmey moḻiyīṟṟil niṉṟatuvum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpi yuṭittu, iṭiyil niṉṟatuvum uyiriṉ iyalpi yuṭittu e-ṟu.
īṟum iṭiyum uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē, mutal meyyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
itaṉāṉ, viḷa mutaliya uyirmey īṟellām akaravīṟu mutaliya uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkip puṇarccipeṟuvaṉa vāyiṉa. varaku eṉpuḻi iṭi niṉṟa rakara uyirmey akaramāy uyirttoṭarmoḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
īṇṭu uyirmey oṟṟumi nayattāṉ uyirmeyyeṉa vēṟu ōr eḻuttāvataṉṟi, īṟum iṭiyum uyireṉa ōreḻuttāyum, mutal meyyeṉa ōreḻuttāyum niṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
ittuṇiyum orumoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ moḻimarapiṉ oḻipāyiṟṟu.	(4)
niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum
108.	uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ(ṟu)
	ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli
	niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ(ṟu)
	āyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci mummoḻip puṇarcciyākātu, irumoḻip puṇarcciyā meṉpatūum, avi eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutalmoḻi varum iṭamum, uyir iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, iv eṉa aṟiya - (eṉṟu ap puṇarccivaki) iviyeṉa aṟiya, kiḷakkum kāli - āciriyar colluṅkālattu, niṟutta col kuṟittu varu kiḷavi eṉṟu a īr iyala - avi niṟutta collum ataṉ poruṇmiyik kuṟittuvarum collumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiyuṭiya, puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - puṇarum nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ā ī, ā valitu, āl ili, āl vīḻntatu eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.
viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ eṉpatu av vurupu kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyi nilimoḻiyuḷ aṭakki irumoḻip puṇarcciyāy niṉṟavāṟu aṟika.	(5)
moḻipuṇar iyalpu mūṉṟu tiripum ōriyalpum ātal
109.	avaṟṟuḷ
	niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
	kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiyap
	peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	mūṉṟē tiripiṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa
	āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci puṇarccivakiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, avi colvakiyāṉ nāṉkā  meṉpatūum, puṇarvatu collum collumēyaṉṟu, eḻuttum eḻuttum eṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - nilimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉappaṭṭa vaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟu āku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyiya - niṟutta colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttiṉōṭu ataṉik kuṟittu varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu peyarc collip puṇarkkum kālattum, peyaroṭu toḻili puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu viṉiccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉic colloṭu peyarccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉiccolloṭu viṉiccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, tiripu mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉa a nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpu - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟum iyalpu oṉṟum ākiya an nāṉkē moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇarum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉ ki, cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṭiyum uriyum peyar viṉikaḷi aṭintallatu tāmāka nillāmiyiṉ, peyar viṉikaṭkē puṇarcci kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(6)
tiripiṉ mūvaki
110.	avitām
	meypiṟi tātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu
	ivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya tiripu mūṉṟum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - at tiriputām, meypiṟitu ātal mikutal kuṉṟal eṉṟu iv eṉa moḻipa tiriyum āṟu - mey piṟitātalum mikutalum kuṉṟalumākiya ivi eṉac colluvar tiriyum neṟiyiṉi e-ṟu.
im mūṉṟum allātatu iyalpeṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	poṟkuṭam, yāṉikkōṭu, maravēr eṉavarum. kuvaḷi malar eṉpatu iyalpu.
ip puṇarcci nāṉkum oru puṇarccikkaṇṇē nikaḻvu peṟumeṉpatu uriyiṟkoḷka.
mēl 'iṭam' (puṇar. 6) eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiripu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, yātāyiṉum oṉṟāyatu, oru puṇarccikku iraṇṭum mūṉṟum oruṅku varappeṟum eṉakkoḷka.
e-ṭu:	'makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ' eṉpatu am mikuti iraṇṭu vantatu, 'nīyir kuṟiyir' eṉpatu am mikuti mūṉṟu vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(7)
niṟuttacollum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum aṭiyoṭum tōṉṟutal
111.	niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya.
itu, "niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi" (puṇar. 5) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum - nili moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉik kuṟittu varucollum, aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya - (tāmē vantu puṇarvataṉṟi), avi yiraṇṭiṉum orōr collaṭivantu oṉṟit tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu,  patiṉāyirat torupaḵtu eṉa varum.
īṇṭu aṭiyeṉṟatu ummittokiyiṉiyum, iru peyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyiṉiyum eṉa vuṇarka. aviyallāta tokikaḷuḷ viṉittokiyum, paṇput tokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ, orucol eṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻit tokiyum taṉakku vēṟu oru muṭipu iṉmiyiṉ orucol eṉappaṭum. iṉi oḻinta vēṟṟumit tokiyum uvamit tokiyum 'taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu orucol eṉappaṭum.
uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum avvāṟē orucol eṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(8)
marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭutal
112.	maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻiyum
	uriyavi uḷavē puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
iḵtu, ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅku iyal moḻiyum - (ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi) marūuttiraḷākiya talitaṭumāṟāka mayaṅkiṉa iyalpi yuṭiya ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiṉa marūu vaḻakkum, uriyavi uḷa puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - uriyaṉa uḷa puṇarum nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mīkaṇ, muṉṟil eṉa varum.
'nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu vaḻakkallā marūu vaḻakkum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉakkoḷka.	(9)
mikkapuṇarcci iruvakittātal
113.	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	eḻuttē cāriyi āyiru paṇpiṉ
	oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kāli.
itu, nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci ittaṉmit teṉpatūum, an nālvakip puṇarcciyum vēṟōrāṟṟāṉ iruvaki yāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟ ṟumiyatu poruṇmiyiṉik kuṟitta puṇarmoḻiyatu nilimiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarmoḻiyatu nilimiyum, eḻuttu cāriyi a iru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - (mikka puṇarccikkaṇ) eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyi mikutalumākiya av viraṇṭu ilakkaṇattāṉum naṭāttutalit tamakku valiyāka uṭiya, puṇarumkāli - avi puṇaruṅkālattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu, makaviṉki, viḷakkuṟitu, paṉiyiṉ kuṟi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
'oḻukkal valiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, eḻuttum cāriyiyum uṭaṉ peṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟukkōṭu, kalattukkuṟi eṉavarum.
alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu, vēṟṟumiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpatu. muṉṉē 'puṇarmoḻi' eṉṟu vittu, 'puṇaruṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarccikkaṇṇē vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāvatu allākkāl, vēṟṟumiyeṉavē paṭumeṉpatu koḷka. īṇṭu 'alvaḻi' yeṉṟatu perumpāṉmiyum eḻuvāyiṉi.	(10)
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum
114.	aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉum
	avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumi yurupē.
itu, mēl vēṟṟumi yeṉṉappaṭṭavaṟṟatu peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum. a āṟu eṉpa vēṟṟumi urupu - av vāṟum eṉṟu colluvar vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷi e-ṟu.
iv vāṟum allaṉa vellām alvaḻi eṉappaṭum. avi eḻuvāyum, viḷiyum, uvamattokiyum, ummittokiyum, paṇput tokiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyum, muṟṟum, iru vaki eccamum, iṭiyum, uriyum eṉa ivi.	(11)
nāṉkaṉurupum ēḻaṉurupum vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumāṟu
115.	valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku
	olvaḻi oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyali nōkkiṟṟoru karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi urupiṟku - valleḻuttu mutalākavuṭiya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiṉa iṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭikkaṇ mikutal vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ, nīrkkaṇ eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
'olvaḻi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉavum koḷka.
'meypiṟi tātal' (puṇar. 7) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcciyum urupu puṇarccikkaṇṇum eytaliṉ, meypiṟitātali eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉi eṭuttōtiya vataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci yallaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka.
e-ṭu:	poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku; nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku eṉa varum.
avaṉkaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉpuḻi iyalpum itaṉāṉē koḷka. kuṉṟiya puṇarcci vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(12)
āṟaṉurupiṉ akaram keṭumiṭam
116.	āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi
	īṟā(ku) akaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, āṟāvataṟkut tokimarapiṉi nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi - āṟām vēṟṟumiyākiya atu eṉṉum colliṭattu akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟu āku akaramuṉi keṭutal vēṇṭum - (neṭumutal kuṟukum) moḻiyīṟṟu uḷatākiṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutal vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	namatu, ematu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉa varum.
mēl īṟāku akaram itaṟkut tārātu, iv vuru pakaramē ēṟi muṭiya amitaliṉ, atu kēṭātal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟa moḻiyākaliṉ ataṉmēl urupakara mēṟi muṭiyal vēṇṭā rāyiṉār pōlu meṉpatu.	(13)
vēṟṟumiyurupu peyarppiṉṉarp puṇartal
117.	vēṟṟumi vaḻiya peyarpuṇar niliyē.
itu, vēṟṟumiyurupu peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi peyar vaḻiya puṇarnili - vēṟṟumi peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉa avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉi, cāttaṉoṭu eṉa varum.
mēl "urupunili tiriyātu īṟupeyark kākum" (vēṟ. 8) eṉṟāraṉṟō veṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyar mutaliya nālvakip puṇarcciyiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkaliṉ, viṉivaḻiyum urupu varumeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatākaliṉ īṇṭu itu kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(14)
peyarnilic cuṭṭu iruvakippaṭutal
118.	uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyareṉṟu
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, vēṟṟumiyoṭu puṇarum peyarkaṭkup peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉṟu a iraṇṭu eṉpa - uyartiṇip peyarum aḵṟiṇip peyarumākiya av viraṇṭumeṉṟu collupa, peyar nilic cuṭṭu - peyarākiya nilimi yatu karuttiṉi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūu, makaṭūu eṉpaṉa uyartiṇip peyar. oṉṟu pala eṉpaṉa aḵṟiṇip peyar.
maṟṟu viravuppeyar kūṟārāyatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ,  maṟṟatu cāttaṉ vantāṉ; vantatu eṉap puṇarccikkaṇ perumpāṉmiyum oru tiṇip pāṟ paṭutaliṉ, av viraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.	(15)
peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉarc cāriyi varutal
119.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyi varumē.
itu, cāriyi varum iṭam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ cāriyi varum - ap peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē cāriyi varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉ ki, makaṭūuviṉ ki, palavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa varum.	(16)
cāriyikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum
120.	avitām
	iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē
	oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē
	aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyi moḻiyē.
itu, cāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟcāriyi yeṉṉappaṭṭavitām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉ eṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa piṟavum - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉ eṉṉum collumākiya av voṉpatum uḷappaṭap piṟavum, aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyi moḻi - ac cāriyiyām taṉmiya eṉṟu colluvar cāriyiyākiya moḻikaḷi e-ṟu.
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, tam, nam, num, um, keḻu, ē, ai, ñāṉṟu eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka.
'eṭutta naṟaviṉ kuliyalaṅ kāntaḷ' (kali. 40) eṉpuḻi alam eṉpatoru cāriyiyum uṇṭāleṉiṉ, ataṉi alaṅku kāntaḷ eṉpataṉ vikārameṉpa.
iṉcāriyi vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum nūluṭ palakālum eṭut tōtappaṭalāṉum, vāḷā ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalāṉum muṉvikkap paṭṭatu. aṉ cāriyiyum atupōlac ciṟappuṭimiyiṉ piṉ vikkap paṭṭatu. iṭi niṉṟavaṟṟiyalpum aṟintu koḷka.	(17)
iṉcāriyiyatu mutal tiriyumāṟu
121.	avaṟṟuḷ
	iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟuti
	muṉṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
itu, avaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cāriyikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - iṉcāriyiyiṉatu ikaram ā eṉṉum collīṟṟumuṉṉar, keṭutalum urittākum – keṭāmiyē yaṉṟik keṭutalum urittām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṉi, āviṉi, āṉkōṭu, āviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.
'muṉṉar' eṉṟataṉāṉ, 'mā' eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉum av viru vitiyum eytum.
e-ṭu:	māṉi, māviṉi, māṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.	(18)
ataṉiṟuti tiriyum iṭam
122.	aḷavāku moḻimutal niliiya uyirmici
	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilittē.
iḵtu, iṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aḷavākum moḻi mutal - aḷavup peyarākum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ, niliiya uyirmici ṉaḵkāṉ - nilipeṟṟa uyirukku mēlāy niṉṟa iṉcāriyiyatu ṉakāram, ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilittu - ṟakāramākiya nilimittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉpuḻi avvāṟu varutal aṟika.
'nilittu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟēḻu eṉa varum.	(19)
vaṟṟuccāriyiyatu mutal tiriyumāṟu
123.	vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ
	aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpē.
itu, vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal aimuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc colmuṉṉar, vaḵkāṉ mey keṭa  aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpu - vaṟṟuccāriyi taṉ vakaramākiya meykeṭa akaram niṟṟalākiya paṇpiṉiyuṭittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi eṉavum, aviyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavum varum.
'ākiya paṇpu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti aikārattoṭu nillātavaḻi, vaṟṟiṉ vakaram akaram niṟkak keṭātu vaṟṟāyē niṟṟal koḷka.
maṟṟitu, "tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu" eṉṉum nayattāṟ keṭātē niṟkumākaliṉ, itu kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ, "cuṭṭu mutal ai īṟṟuc colliṉ aimuṉ" eṉa ōtātu, "cuṭṭu mutal aimmuṉ" eṉa ac col muṉ ellām keṭuvatupōla ōtiṉamiyiṉ, vēṇṭiṟṟeṉpatu.	(20)
ṉakarīṟṟuc cāriyikaḷatu īṟu tiriyum iṭam
124.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ nāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
itu, ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyi nāṉkaṟkum īṟu tiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ urupiṟku ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ - nāṉkām urupiṟku (ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyiyellām) ṉakaram ṟakaramām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum.	(21)
āṉcāriyi īṟu tiriyum iṭam
125.	āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟē
	nāḷmuṉ varūum valmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu, āṉcāriyi poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ īṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷmuṉ varum val mutal toḻiṟku āṉiṉ ṉakaramum - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻutti mutalākavuṭiya viṉic coṟkaṇ varum āṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum, ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - an nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyiyōṭu oru taṉmittāy ṉakāram ṟakaramām e-ṟu.
'toḻiṟku' eṉpataṉit 'toḻiṟkaṇ' eṉa mayakkamākak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
ummiyi iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāṉē etiratu taḻīiya tākki, ataṉāṉ nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakāramāyt tirital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiriyumeṉa, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiritalum tiriyāmiyumuṭiya teṉpatu  ñāpakattāṟ koḷḷap paṭum.
e-ṭu:	paṟampiṟ pāri, vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	(22)
attuccāriyiyatu akaram keṭumiṭam
126.	attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi yilli.
iḵtu, attu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi illi - attuc cāriyiyiṉ akaram akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉṉar illiyākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makattukki eṉa varum.	(23)
ikkuc cāriyiyatu ikaram keṭumiṭam
127.	ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉi yaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ikkuc cāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉi aṟṟu - ikkuc cāriyiyiṉatu ikaram ikara īṟṟuccol muṉṉar mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōlak keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
aikāra īṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum av vikaram keṭutal
128.	aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal niliyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyiṉ muṉṉarum a iyal niliyum - (ikkiṉ ikaram) ikaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉaṉṟi aikāravīṟṟuc col muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(25)
akkuc cāriyiyatu īṟu tiriyum iṭam
129.	eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	akkiṉ iṟutimeym miciyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu, akkīṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e peyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - evvakippaṭṭa peyar muṉṉum valleḻuttu varumiṭattu akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, mey miciyoṭum keṭum - ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa valleḻuttākiya mey taṉakku mēl niṉṟa meyyoṭum keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuṉṟakkūki, maṉṟappeṇṇi, īmakkuṭam, aracak kaṉṉi, tamiḻakkūttu eṉavarum.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi maṟṟak kaṇaṅkaṭkum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tamiḻanūl, tamiḻayāppu, tamiḻavariyar eṉa varum.	(26)
am cāriyiyatu īṟu tiriyumāṟu
130.	ammiṉ iṟuti kacatak kālit
	taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum.
itu, ammiṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ammiṉ iṟuti ka ca tak kāli - ammiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu ka ca takkaḷ varumoḻiyāka vanta kālattu, taṉ mey tirintu ṅa ña na ākum - taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṅa ña nakkaḷām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl eṉa varum.
'taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ iṟuti makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti makaramum tiriyumeṉpatu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellārtaṅkiyum, ellānaṅkiyum, ellīr nuṅkiyum, 'vāṉa varivillun tiṅkaḷum', eṉa varum.	(27)
av īṟu iyalpām iṭam
131.	meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varūum kāli
	iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varum kāli - ammiṉ iṟuti meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu; iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - iṉṟimuṭitali vēṇṭum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiya ñeri; nuṉi, muri, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa  varum.
uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷiyavili eṉavum uyir varuvaḻik kēṭum koḷka. puḷiyili eṉa muḻuvatūum kēṭu kūṟalum koḷka.	(28)
iṉ cāriyi muṟṟum keṭum iṭam
132.	iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku
	iṉṉeṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iṉ cāriyi muḻuvatūum keṭum iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ eṉa varum vēṟṟumi urupiṟku - iṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, iṉ eṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉum cāriyitāṉ iṉṟi muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ eṉa varum.
'avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉ ikaram' (puṇar. 18) eṉṟataṉ piṉ viyātataṉāl, ciṟupāṉmi iṉ cāriyi keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ eṉa varum.	(29)
cāriyikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam;
oruvurupu cāriyi peṟutalum peṟāmiyum uṭittātal
133.	peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicippa
	vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
	coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā(tu)
	iṭiniṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyi iyaṟki
	uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum.
itu, cāriyikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peyarum toḻilum pirintu oruṅku icippa - peyarccollum viṉiccollum peyarum viṉiyumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyum icippa, vēṟṟumi urupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumi yurupu tokātu nilipeṟṟa iṭattiṉum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutikkaṇṇum - avi tōṟṟutal vēṇṭāta tokutikkaṇṇum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, col citar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyi peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭiniṉṟu iyalum - avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vantu viḷaṅkātu avaṟṟiṭiyē niṉṟu naṭakkum, cāriyi iyaṟki - cāriyiyiṉ iyalpu. uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum - avitām uṇṭātalum illiyātalum oṭu urupiṉiṭattu okkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ, viḷaviṉik kuṟittavaṉ eṉavum, nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉavum varum.
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkaṉmiyiṉ, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉpaṉa cāriyi peṟāvāyiṉa. ellā nammiyum eṉac cāriyi īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ, iṭiniṉṟiyaṟal perumpāṉmi yeṉak koḷka.
pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, pūvoṭu virinta kūntaleṉa vum, uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottavāṟu.
iyaṟki eṉṟataṉāṉ oṭuvurupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟutalum peṟāmiyum oḻiya, orōvaḻip peṟṟē varumeṉpatu koḷka--
e-ṭu:	palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(30)
attuccāriyi vaṟṟuccāriyi varumiṭattu
nilivarumoḻikaḷ puṇarnili
134.	attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
	oṟṟumey keṭutal teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
	avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, attum vaṟṟum varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyiṉum varumoḻiyiṉum varuñ ceyki kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attē vaṟṟē a irumoḻimēl oṟṟu mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya av virumoḻi mēlniṉṟa oṟṟut taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟu muṉvarum valleḻuttu mikum - av virucāriyi muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattukkuṟi, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum.
kalaṉ eṉa, ṉakāramāka niṟuttik keṭukka.
'oṉṟiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷḷiyīṟalvaḻi vikāravaki yāṉ niṉṟaṉavum avaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukkappaṭumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. iḵtu aikāra īṟu.
īṇṭu 'valleḻuttu miku'meṉṟatu, īṟṟu valleḻuttiṉṟit tirintu muṭiyum ṉakaramum ṇakaramum lakaramum ḷakaramum eṉa ivaṟṟi nōkkiyeṉa vuṇarka.
attu muṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, attiṉ mici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟkavum peṟum.
e-ṭu:	viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
'mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram piṟavuyiriṉ muṉṉum keṭutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aṇṇāttēri eṉavarum.
'teṟṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(31)
eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum
135.	kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
	nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu, eḻuttuc cāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu porunti, nērattōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi - ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭaṉpaṭat tōṉṟum eḻuttuccāriyi yātaṟku e-ṟu.
'nērat tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nērattōṉṟātaṉa āṉam, ēṉam ōṉam, eṉa ivi.	(32)
karamum kāramum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟāmi
136.	avaṟṟuḷ
	karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyi cila eḻuttoṭu vārā eṉa eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttu ila - mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku ila. kāram neṭṭeḻuttiṟku uṇṭu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ākāram, īkāram eṉa varum.	(33)
kuṟṟeḻuttu muccāriyiyum peṟutal
137.	varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭiya.
itu, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyi vilakkiṉamiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum cila vilakkuvatuṇṭukol eṉṉum aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭiya - varalāṟṟu muṟimiyiyuṭiya mūṉṟu cāriyiyum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	akāram, akaram, aḵkāṉ eṉa varum.
'varaṉmuṟi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytamikku muṭiyumeṉpatu koḷka.	(34)
ai, au - iraṇṭum kāṉcāriyiyum peṟutal
138.	aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ kāramuṅ kāṉum eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aikāram aukāram kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷil aikāramum aukāramum muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aikāṉ, aukāṉ eṉa varum.	(35)
uṭalmēl uyirvan toṉṟutal
139.	puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu
	meyyoṭuñ civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē.
iḵtu, uyirmutalmoḻi puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ varuṅkāṟ piṟappa toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi īṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum civaṇum a iyal keṭuttu - ap puḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum tāṉ taṉiniṉṟa av viyalpiṉik keṭuttu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āl + aṭi = ālaṭi eṉa varum.
'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, iyalpallāta puḷḷimuṉ uyirvantālum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ataṉi eṉavum, nāṭuri eṉavum varum.
puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉum eṉa, tokuttu niṉṟa ummiyi virittata ṉāṉē kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nākaritu eṉa varum.	(36)
uyir nīṅkiyaviṭattu mey puḷḷipeṟutal
140.	meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru vākum.
itu, puṇarcciyiṭattu uyirmey uyir  nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuva toru viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉ uru ākum - mey taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu nīṅkiya vaḻit taṉ puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉi - ataṉ + ai eṉa varum. 	(37)
uṭampaṭumey tōṉṟum iṭam
141.	ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
	uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal variyār.
iḵtu, uyirīṟu uyirmutaṉ moḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻva toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā moḻikkum - mūvakippaṭṭa moḻikkum, uyir varu vaḻi - uyir mutaṉmoḻi varum iṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal variyār - iṭi uṭampaṭumey vaṭivu kōṭali nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, eruvaṅkuḻi, viḷavattukkoṭkum eṉa varum.
'uriyiṟ kōṭal' eṉpataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumeyyāvaṉa yakaramum vakaramum eṉak koḷka. ikaravīṟum īkāravīṟum aikāravīṟum yakaravuṭam paṭumey koḷvaṉa; allaṉa vellām vakaramey koḷvaṉa.
'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, vikārappaṭṭa moḻik kaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka.
e-ṭu:	maravaṭi, āyirutiṇi eṉa varum.
'variyār' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutali aṉṟeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illi eṉa varum.	(38)
puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōcivēṟupāṭṭāl puṇarcci vēṟupaṭumāṟu
142.	eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
	iciyiṉ tirital niliiya paṇpē.
iḵtu, eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuttu ōr aṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmiyāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅkiniṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, iciyiṉ tirital niliiya paṇpu - ōci vēṟṟumiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilipeṟṟa paṇpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa varum. 	(39)
mēliccūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi
143.	avitām
	muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
	iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟcolliya puṇarmoḻikaḷtām, muṉṉap poruḷa - muṉṉattiṉāṉ uṇarum poruṇmiyi yuṭiya; puṇarcci vāyiṉ iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ila - avi puṇarcci yiṭattu ittaṉmiya veṉṉum eḻuttu muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla e-ṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟavaḻip poṉṉārāycci uḷ vaḻip poṉṉeṉavum, cempārāycci uḷvaḻic cempeṉavum kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu.
maṟṟu, itaṉmēl 'iciyiṉ tirital' (eḻut. 142) eṉa aṟiyumāṟu kūṟiṉāraṉṟōveṉiṉ, ōci eṉṟamiyāṉ aḵtu oliyeḻuttiṟ keṉavum, "iṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttuk kaṭaṉila" eṉṟataṉāṉ itu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)

puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

5
tokimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, mēl akattōtti ṉuḷ irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉum virintu muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyellām tokuttu muṭittaliṉ tokimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēl puṇariyaluṭ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāṟ ceykikūṟum vaḻi, tokkup puṇaruñ ceyki kūṟaliṟ puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
varumoḻi kacatapa-kkaḷiṉ muṉṉar mikum melleḻuttukkaḷ
144.	kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum
	melleḻut tiyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
	ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟā kummē
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, uyirmayaṅkiyaliyum (218), puḷḷimayaṅkiyaliyum (301, 315) nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ca ta pa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu iyaṟki - uyirīṟṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum iru vaḻiyum ka ca ta pakkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷiṉmēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpukūṟiṉ, colliya muṟiyāṉ ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum - melleḻuttu mēṟcollum muṟimiyāṉ ka ca ta pakkaḷukku niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉē ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉ āṉa - at taṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya moḻikaḷiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
'tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉavum avvāṟē tirintu melleḻuttā meṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyaṉmiyiṉ, viḷakkuṟumi eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.	(1)
iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇam oḻintakaṇaṅkaḷ iyalpāy muṭital
145.	ñanama yaveṉum mutalāku moḻiyum
	uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉittum ellā vaḻiyum
	niṉṟa coṉmuṉ iyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, vaṉkaṇamoḻinta kaṇaṅkaṭku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma ya va eṉum mutal āku moḻiyum - ña na ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷavākum moḻiyum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa - uyirmutalākiya moḻiyumāka, aṉṟi aṉittum ellā vaḻiyum - av vaṉittu moḻiyum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellāviṭattum, niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpākum - irupattunāṉku īṟṟup peyarākiya nilimoḻi muṉṉar iyalpāki muṭiyum e-ṟu.
īṇṭu 'uḷappaṭa'veṉpatu āka veṉṉum poruṇmittu.
e-ṭu:	viḷa, tāḻ eṉa niṟutti ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu, aṭintatu, āṭiṟṟu, iṭintatu, īriṟṟu, uṭintatu, ūṟiṟṟu, eḻuntatu, ēṟiṟṟu, aitu, oṭintatu, ōṭiṟṟu, auviyattatu, nuntiyatu eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimi, aṭivu, āṭṭam, iṭipu,  īṭṭam, uṭipu, ūṟṟam, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oṭukkam, ōkkam, auviyam, nunti eṉavum oṭṭikkoḷka.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭalum, uṭampaṭumey kōṭalum, uyirēṟi muṭitalum eṉavarum ikkaruvittiripu mūṉṟum tiripu aṉmiyiṉ tiripeṉap paṭāveṉpatu koḷka. iḵtu irupattu nāṉku īṟṟiṟkum, alvaḻiyiṉum, vēṟṟumiyiṉum, akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttirattāṉ muṭivataṉi, īṇṭu oru cūttirattāṉ tokuttu muṭittatāyiṟṟu. mēlum ivvāṟē tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟa vāṟu aṟika.	(2)
ammuṭipu cilavaṟṟiṟku ēlāmi
146.	avaṟṟuḷ 
	melleḻut tiyaṟki yuṟaḻiṉum variyār
	colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟuti yāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku, am muṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉum, melleḻuttu iyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār - melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpu iyalpātalē yaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭar moḻi īṟṟukkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	katirñeri, katirññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum, īreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku uṟaḻcci muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pūñeri, pūññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum; kāyñeri, kāyññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum varum.
'colliya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum īreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku mikku muṭital koḷka.
eṅiṭu:	kiññerittār; nīṭṭiṉār, *muṟittār eṉavum, meyñ ñāṉam, meynnūl, meymmaṟantār eṉavum varum.(3)
*(pāṭam) 'maṟittār' eṉpatu.
yāvum ñāvum viṉiyāka varuvaḻi orutaṉmiyavām iṭam
147.	ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
	viṉiyō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakaraṅkaḷ mutalāmvaḻi nikaḻvatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ṉa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉmuṉ, yāvum ñāvum viṉi ōr aṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - yāvum ñāvum viṉiccoṟkaṇ mutalātaṟku oru taṉmiya veṉṟu colluvar *pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇyātta maṇñātta eṉavum, poṉyātta  poṉñātta eṉavum varum.
ñā muṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, yā mutaṉ moḻikkaṇ ñā varumeṉṟu koḷka.	(4)
*(pāṭam) 'āciriyar' eṉpatu.
ṇakara ṉakara īṟukaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyāmi
148.	moḻimuta lākum ellā eḻuttum
	varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟṟiṟkum ṉakāravīṟṟiṟkum alvaḻikkaṇ nili moḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻimutal ākum ellā eḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟu collappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumiṭattu, niṉṟa a iru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻi tiripu iṭaṉ ila - *mēṟ colliniṉṟa ṇa ṉakkaḷākiya avviru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattut tiriyum iṭam ila e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa vaṉkaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum avvāṟē oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi tiripum uṇṭeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cāṭkōl eṉa varum.	(5)
*(pāṭam) 'peyarc colliṉiṉṟa' eṉpatu.
vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum iyalpukaṇam varuvaḻi
avvīṟukaḷ tiriyāmi
149.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki yāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av viraṇṭu īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkaṇ nilimoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu alvaḻi - valleḻuttu allāta viṭattu, mēlkūṟu iyaṟki āvayiṉ āṉa - mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām av viraṇṭu puḷḷiyiṭattum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṟci, nīṭci eṉa vaṉkaṇam oḻittu ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(6)
lakara ṉakara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉart takara nakara varumoḻikaḷ tiriyumāṟu
150.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	tana eṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi muṉṉar - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉa variṉ - ta na eṉa varumoḻi variṉ, ṟa ṉa ākum - at takara nakaraṅkaḷ niraṉiṟiyāṉē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḵṟītu, kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu, poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(7)
ṇakara ḷakara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar avi tiriyumāṟu
151.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ḷa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar (atikārattiṉāṉ takāra nakāraṅkaḷ variṉ), ṭa ṇa eṉat tōṉṟum - avi niraṉiṟiyāṉ ṭakāra ṇakaraṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: 	maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu, muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(8)
muṉṉili viṉiccol vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
152.	uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	iyalpā kunavum uṟaḻpā kunavumeṉṟu
	āyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu, muṉṉili viṉiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilic coṟkaḷum puḷḷi iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉilic coṟkaḷum, valleḻuttu variṉ - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ, iyalpu ākunavum uṟaḻpu ākunavum eṉṟu a īr iyala - iyalpāvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāvaṉavum eṉa av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya e-ṟu.
uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟamiyāṉ, muṉṉili viṉiccol eṉpatu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	eṟikoṟṟā, koṇākoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkoṟṟā, tiṉkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivi iyalpu. 
	naṭakoṟṟā, naṭakkoṟṟā eṉavum, īrkoṟṟā, īrkkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivi uṟaḻcci.	(9)
mēlimuṭipu cila īṟukaṭku ēlāmi
153.	auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum
	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyum
	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyum uḷappaṭa
	muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku am muṭipu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) au eṉa varum uyir iṟu collum - au eṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷiiṟutiyum - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟuti yum uḷappaṭa - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya collumākiya ivi, muṉṉili moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉili moḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻvumākiya muṭiviṟku muṟṟattōṉṟā e-ṟu.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu oḻittu, oḻintaṉavellām nilimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu, varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitalum, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kuvukoṟṟā, kuvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā, porunukoṟṟā, porunukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā, tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā, tevvukkoṟṟā eṉavum; kūṭṭukoṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum. 	(10)
uyartiṇippeyar nāṉku kaṇattum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
154.	uyirī ṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum
	ellā vaḻiyum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, uyartiṇip peyar nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum, iru vaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum - uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi iṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum - puḷḷi iṟutiyiyuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - nāṉku kaṇattilum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellāviṭattum iyalpām eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nampi eṉavum avaṉ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṇ kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāvaṉ, valiyaṉ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ, auviyattāṉ eṉavum; vēṟṟumikkaṇ ki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; yāppu, valimi eṉavum; aṭipu, auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. oruveṉ eṉa niṟutti, kuṟiyeṉ, ciṟiyeṉ eṉavum; ki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum taṉmip peyarkkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
'uyirīṟu', 'puḷḷiyiṟuti' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇip poruḷ iyalpaṉṟi muṭivaṉa vellām koḷka.
e-ṭu:	palcāṉṟār, kapilaparaṇar, iṟivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar, pallaracar eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu muṭintaṉa. kōlikakkaruvi, vaṇṇārappeṇṭir, ācīvakap paḷḷi eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam, kumarakkōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam, piramak kōṭṭam eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇi(viṉi)ccol iyalpāyum tirintum muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu: uṇpa cāṉṟār, uṇṭār cāṉṟār, uṇpēṉ pārppēṉ eṉa ivi iyalpu. *uṇṭēñ cāṉṟēm, uṇṭēnām eṉa ivi tirintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
*(pāṭam) 'uṇṭaṇem cāṉṟēm' eṉpatu.
ikarīṟ ṟuyartiṇip peyar mikka tiripu peṟutal
155.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ikarīṟ ṟuppeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu, uyartiṇip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyartiṇip peyaruḷ, ikara īṟṟup peyar tiripu iṭaṉ uṭittu - ikaramākiya iṟutiyi yuṭiya peyar tirintu muṭiyum iṭamum uṭittu e-ṟu.
uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, itu mikka tiripeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	eṭṭippū, eṭṭippuravu; kāvitippū, kāvitippuravu; nampippū, nampippēṟu eṉa varum.
'iṭaṉuṭittu' eṉpataṉāṉ, ikara īṟaṉṟip piṟa īṟum īṟu tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉa īṇṭukkoḷka. 
e-ṭu: tiṉippū, tiṉippuravu eṉa varum.	(12)
viravuppeyar muṭiyumāṟu
156.	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, viravuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpum uḷa - uyar tiṇi yōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravum viravuppeyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa; iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa e-ṟu.
iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa, mēl (348-52) akattōttiṉuṭ kūṟappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉkuṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉkuṟitu eṉavum; cāttaṉki eṉavum; ivvāṟu alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka.	(13)
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tiripu
157.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṉ
	tammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ
	meymmi yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	ammuṟi iraṇṭum uriyavi uḷavē
	vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tiripu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṉ - valleḻuttiṉatu mikuti mēṟcollum muṟimiyāṉ, tammiṉ ākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ - am mūṉṟāvataṉ poru ḷākiya viṉimutaṟ poruḷkaḷ tammāṉ uḷavākiya viṉic coṟkaḷ tām avaṟṟumuṉvariṉ, meymmiyākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum a muṟi iraṇṭum uriyavi uḷa - iyalpākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟutalu mākiya am muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭu ceykiyum uriyaṉa uḷa, vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṉ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum - avaṟṟi mēlē vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu collumiṭattup pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi iyalpu. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭ paṭṭāṉ, vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
'puḷḷiyīṟu uyirīṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ ekarappēṟu koḷka. 'uriyavi yuḷa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilimoḻiyoṟṟut tiriyāmiyum tiritalum koḷka.	(14)
iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tiripu
158.	melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum
	uyirmika varuvaḻi yuyirkeṭa varutalum
	cāriyi yuḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭutalum
	cāriyi yuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyalum
	cāriyi yiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum
	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyark kavviya ṉiliyalum
	meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟki yātalum
	aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikkum
	aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumittiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu valleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟutalum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu melleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟutalum, iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum - iyaṟkiyiṭattu mikuti tōṉṟutalum, uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭa varutalum - uyir mika varumiṭattu av vuyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻi cāriyi keṭutalum - cāriyiyuḷḷa iṭattuc cāriyi keṭutalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻi taṉ urupu niliyalum - cāriyi uḷḷaviṭattuc cāriyiyoṭu taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum, cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - cāriyi peṟuka eṉṟavaḻic cāriyi peṟātu iyal pākiya moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyiṉum nilimoḻiyiṉum mikkum tirintum varum varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟuta lum, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - uyartiṇip peyariṭattut taṉṉurupu tokātē virintu varutalum, aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku a iyal niliyalum - uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravumpeyarkkum av vurupu av viyalpilē niṟṟalum, meypiṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟki ātalum - mey piṟitāy muṭiyumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭitalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - attaṉmiyaṉa piṟa muṭipukaḷum, taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa kiḷantu poruḷ varintu icikkum - taṉakku iyalpākiya kūṟṟāṉ akattōttiṉuṭ poruḷ peṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭiviṉit tāṉ varintu vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripu eṉa moḻipa - aikāra vēṟṟumiyiṉatu vēṟupāṭṭup puṇarcci eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷakkuṟittāṉ eṉpatu melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi (218) valleḻuttumikkatu. maraṅkuṟittāṉ eṉpatu val leḻuttu mikuvaḻi (311) melleḻuttu mikkatu. tāyk koli eṉpatu iyalpāmiṭattu (359) mikkatu. palāk kuṟittāṉ eṉpatu uyirmika varuvaḻi (227) uyir keṭṭatu. vaṇṭukoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyi uḷvaḻic (421) cāriyi keṭṭatu. vaṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyi uḷvaḻit (421) taṉṉurupu niliyiṟṟu. puḷi kuṟittāṉ, puḷikkuṟittāṉ, 1pūlkuṟittāṉ, pūṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa ivi (245, 376) cāriyi yiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟiṉa. nampiyik koṇarntāṉ (157) eṉpatu uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu vantatu. koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu viravuppeyarkku av viyal niliyatu. maṇkoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu meypiṟitākiṭattu (303) iyaṟkiyāy vantatu.
'aṉṉa piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvaṉa: kaḻikuṟittāṉ, 2tiṉipiḷantāṉ eṉpaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'oḻiyātu' eṉṟataṉāṉ orōvaḻi oḻintum varum.
e-ṭu:	'avaṟkaṇṭu' (akam. 48) eṉavum, 'oṉṉārt teṟalum' (kuṟaḷ. 264) eṉavum ivi uyartiṇiyuḷ oḻintu vantaṉa. makaṟpeṟṟāṉ, makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉa ivi viravup peyaruḷ oḻintu vantaṉa.
iv vilēcutaṉṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumiyum koḷka.
'meypeṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	mikoṇarntāṉ, mikkoṇarntāṉ eṉavum; vilkōḷ, viṟkōḷ eṉavum varum.
ivvāṟu tirintu muṭiyātu, akattōttiṉ potumuṭipē (255) muṭipāyk kaṭukkuṟittāṉ, ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa muṭivaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmiyum iraṇṭāvatu viṉiyoṭu muṭiyumvaḻip pōlum. atu "tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ coṉ muṉvariṉ" (tokimarapu 14) eṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṟ koḷḷa vittār pōlum.
'taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumi viṉi yoṭu muṭiyumvaḻi iyalpum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'varipāy varuṭi' (malipaṭu. 503), 'pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷi' (puṟam. 258) eṉa varum.	(15)
1(pāṭam) 'ālkuṟittāṉ' 'āṟkuṟittāṉ' eṉpatu.
2(pāṭam)  'paṉipiḷantāṉ' eṉpatu.
ikara aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkaṭku alvaḻi muṭipu
159.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
	īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliiya
	avitām
	iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
	uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkkum aikāravīṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi i ai eṉṉum īṟṟup peyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattu i ai eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvaki muṭipu nilimi yuṭiya, avitām iyalpākunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟa ḻākunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avitām iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum val leḻuttu mikuvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum iviyeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	parutti kuṟitu, 1ari kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa ivi iyalpu. alikkoṟṟaṉ, 2pulikkoṟṟaṉ eṉa ivi mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu, kiḷikkuṟitu, tiṉikuṟitu, tiṉikkuṟitu eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
'peyarkkiḷavi mūvaki niliya' eṉavē, peyarkkiḷavi yallāta kiḷavi mikutiyum iyalpum eṉa iruvakiya eṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ollik koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu viṉiccol mikuti. ikaravīṟṟu mikuti vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. "tillic collē" (col. 253) "maṉṉic collē" (col. 252) eṉṟatu aikāravīṟ ṟiṭiccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. kaṭikā eṉpatu ikaravīṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. iv vīṟṟumikuti vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. paṇittōḷ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu uriccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(16)
1(pāṭam) 'kari kuṟitu' eṉpatu.
2(pāṭam) 'pulikkoṟṟaṉ' eṉpatu.
avvīrīṟṟu iṭiccol cilavaṟṟatu muṭipu
160.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya ikara iṟutiyum
	ekaramutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum
	cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻā kunavum
	colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vikara aikāra vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya moḻimutal viṉāviṉuṭiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aieṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa aikāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉi mutaṟkaṇṇuṭiya aikāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷa eṉa moḻipa - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa vum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē uḷaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, utōḷik koṇṭāṉ, etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum;
	āṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ, īṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, ūṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivi mikku muṭintaṉa.
avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ; ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; uvvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkavikoṇṭāṉ, āṅkavikkoṇṭāṉ; īṅkivi koṇṭāṉ, īṅkivikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ūṅkuvikoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuvik koṇṭāṉ yāṅkavi koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavik koṇṭāṉ  eṉavum ivi uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aikāravīṟṟuḷ uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa tiripuṭiyaṉa. tiripillaṉa peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'colliya maruṅku' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa aikāravīṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṇṭic cāṉṟār, orutiṅkaḷik kuḻavi eṉa varum.	(17)
neṭṭeḻuttiṉ muṉṉarniṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum, kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum
161.	neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutalum
	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru viraṭṭalum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar taṉ uruvu iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭutalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟi iyal eṉpa - ivi aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟiya muṟimiyāṉa iyalpuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōṟītu, kōṉaṉṟu eṉa ivi neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭaṉa. 
	maṇṇakal, poṉṉakal eṉa ivi kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuruvu iraṭṭiṉa.
mēliccūttirattu (162) āṟaṉurupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, oṟṟu iraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻik kaṇṇateṉṟu koḷka.
kuṟiyatu piṉkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, kuṟiyatu tirintu neṭiyatāyavaḻi ataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāyk keṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tammi, nammi eṉa ivi neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭiṉa. maṟṟiyatu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'aṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvatu takāra nakāraṅkaḷ vantu tirintavaḻi yeṉpatūum, āṇṭu ellām keṭā teṉpatūum koḷka. tēṉṟītu eṉpatu āṇṭuk keṭātatu.
'neṟiyiyal' eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟiṉṟip puṇarcciyāṟ peṟṟatum iraṭṭumeṉa vuṇarka. 
e-ṭu:	avvaṭi eṉavarum.	(18)
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu iraṭṭā iṭam
162.	āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum
	kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli
	īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum
	neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, urupiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum īṟu āku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum - āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇṇum īṟāku puḷḷikaḷ akarattoṭu nilipeṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻi muṉ kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal illi - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum moḻikkaṇ mēṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tamatu, tamakku; namatu, namakku eṉavarum.
'kūṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉē neṭumutal kuṟukāta moḻikku iv viru vitiyum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellār tamatum, ellār tamakkum eṉa varum.	(19)
ṉanumṉa iṟuti oṟṟum aṟṟātal
163.	nummeṉ iṟutiyum annili tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟutiyum a nili tiriyātu - num eṉṉum makaraviṟuti mēṟkūṟiya īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum kuṟṟoṟṟiraṭṭāmiyumākiya annilimiyiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	numakku, numatu eṉa varum.	 (20)
yakaramum uyirum varumiṭattu iyalpāka niṟkum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ
164.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
	yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi iṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu av vukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ yāṉā, uriñ aṉantā; porun yāṉā, porun aṉantā; uriñ ātā, porun ātā eṉa oṭṭuka.	(21)
aḷavu niṟi eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu
165.	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāki
	aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa ellāc collum
	tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
	mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
	otta teṉpa ēyeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum cuṭṭi uḷa eṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāy aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyum karuti uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tam tam kiḷavi tam akappaṭṭa muttivarum kālam tōṉṟiṉ ē eṉ cāriyi ottatu eṉpa - tamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tamakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyuḷḷaṉa tam muṉṉē varum kālam tōṉṟumāyiṉ ē eṉṉuñ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalip poruntiṟṟu eṉpa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa ivi aḷavup peyar; toṭiyēkaḵcu, koḷḷēaiyavi eṉa ivi niṟip peyar; kāṇiyēmuntiriki, kālēkāṇi eṉa ivi eṇṇup peyar.
'uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉē, ē eṉ cāriyi peṟātaṉavum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi eṉa varum.	(22)
ṉaariṉa varuvaḻi, mēli ēkāraccāriyi peṟāmi
166.	ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikkup
	puriva taṉṟāl cāriyi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ari eṉa varum pālvari kiḷavikkup purivatu aṉṟu cāriyi iyaṟki - ari eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟi yuṇara niṉṟa colliṟkup poruntuvataṉṟu mēṟkūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyi peṟum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkari, toṭiyari, oṉṟari eṉa varum.
itu 'oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku' (puṇar. 30) aṉṟu eṉpataṉāṉ vilakkuṇṇātōveṉil, 'tammakappaṭṭa' eṉṟu varumoḻiyiyum varintōtiṉamiyiṉ itaṟku av viti cellā teṉpatu.	(23)
ṉakuṟiṉa varuvaḻi, vēṟṟumippuṇarcci muṭipu peṟutal
167.	kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
	niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟi eṉ kiḷavi muṉ varu kāli - kuṟi eṉṉum col aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi iyaṟki - niṟiyat tōṉṟum mēl (236) vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipiṟkuc collum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: urikkuṟi, kalakkuṟi; toṭikkuṟi, koṭkuṟi; kāṇikkuṟi, kāṟkuṟi eṉa varum.
'muṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum vēṟṟumi muṭipu eytukaveṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	kalappayaṟu eṉa varum.
'niṟiya' eṉpataṉāṉ, kūṟu eṉpataṉkaṇṇum vēṟṟumi muṭipu eytumeṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	nāḻikkūṟu eṉa varum.	(24)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu iṉcāriyi peṟutal
168.	kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu, vēṟṟumi muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarakku - kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku, cāriyi iṉ - kuṟiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi varum cāriyi iṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkiṉkuṟi, kaḻañciṉkuṟi, oṉṟiṉkuṟi eṉa varum.	(25)
ṉakalamṉa attuccāriyi peṟutal
169.	attiṭi varūuṅ kalameṉ aḷavē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kalam eṉ aḷavu iṭi attu varum - kalam eṉṉum aḷavup peyar (kuṟiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi) iṭiyil attu varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattukkuṟi eṉa varum.
itu, 'kalaṉ' eṉṉum ṉakaravīṟēl, nilimoḻi oṟṟukkēṭum, varumoḻi oṟṟuppēṟum 'attē vaṟṟē' (puṇar. 31) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
'kalaṉeṉ aḷavu' eṉa ōtātatu ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkkip pōlum.
cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ (167) eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(26)
ṉapaṉiṉayum ṉakāṉavum iṉcāriyi peṟutal
170.	paṉiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟiyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉi eṉ aḷavum kā eṉ niṟiyum - paṉi eṉṉum aḷavup peyarum kā eṉṉum niṟip peyarum, niṉiyum kāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - (kuṟi eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) ārāyuṅ kālattu iṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyiṉ kuṟi, kāviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.
'niṉayuṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttup pēṟum ciṟu pāṉmi koḷka. (eḻut. 167)
e-ṭu:	paṉikkuṟi, kākkuṟi eṉa varum.	(27)
aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttu variyaṟi
171.	aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimuta lāki
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa  oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
	avitām
	kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā
	akara ukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttukku variyaṟi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷa eṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟu eḻuttu - aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum moḻi mutalāy uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttu, avitām- avi (yāviyōveṉiṉ), ka ca ta pa eṉṟā na ma va eṉṟā akaramōṭu ukaramōṭu avi eṉa moḻipa - ka ca ta pa kkaḷum na ma va kkaḷum akaramum ukaramumākiya avi eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟi, mā, vari, anti eṉavum varum.
'uḷaveṉap paṭṭa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uḷaveṉap paṭātaṉavum aḷavi uḷaveṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	immi, ōraṭi, ōreṭi eṉa varum.
maṟṟu iv variyaṟi kūṟip payantatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ (390) avaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟum vaḻi, atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu, oḻinta kaṇattiṉum cēṟaṟku eṉpatu. (28)
ivviyaṟkup puṟaṉaṭi
172.	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉ iviivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
	kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
	meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
	ottavi uriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.
iḵtu, iv vōttup puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) īṟuiyal maruṅkiṉ - uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāṉavi varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivi ivaṟṟu iyalpu eṉa kūṟiya kiḷavi pal āṟu ellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ (avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātu niṉṟa) palavaki muṭipukaḷellām, mey talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi ottavi uriya puṇarmoḻinili - uṇmiyit talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭip poruntiṉavi uriyavām puṇarum moḻikaḷ nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷa ñāṉṟatu eṉpuḻi nilimoḻip peyaratu iyalpum, 'ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟu' (puṟam. 82) eṉa nili moḻi viṉiyāya vaḻi irumoḻi iyalpumākiya 'ña na ma ya va' (toki marapu 2) eṉpataṉ oḻipum,
	maṇ kaṭitu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattu iyalpākiya 'moḻimuta lākum' (toki marapu 5) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, naṭañeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu irumoḻi iyalpum, nilkoṟṟā, niṟkoṟṟā eṉa nilimoḻi tirinta uṟaḻcciyum, tukkoṟṟā, tuññeḷḷā eṉṉum mikutiyum, uriñuñeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu ukarap pēṟum, uriñyāṉā, uriñ aṉantā eṉṉum irumoḻi iyalpum, maṇṇukoṟṟā, maṇṇuk koṟṟā, ñeḷḷā eṉavarum 'auveṉa varūum' (toki. 10) eṉpataṉuḷ vilakkap paṭāta ṇakara ṉakara lakara ḷakara mākiya puḷḷiyiṟuti kaḷiṉ nilimoḻi ukarap pēṟumākiya "uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷavi" (toki. 9) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, viravuppeyart tiripiṉ mēl eṭuttu ōtappaṭātaṉavāya niṉñāṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varum.
'aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar' (toki. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla alvaḻi muṭipākiya 'vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi' (toki. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, patakku nāṉāḻi, cīrakari, orumāvari eṉṉum "uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki" (toki. 22) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, piṟavaṟṟiṉ oḻipumellām īṇṭē koḷka.(29)
yāvar, yātu - eṉpavaṟṟatu marūumuṭipu
173.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
	peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalum ēṉi
	oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
	oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum iraṇṭum
	maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ tiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu, marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palar aṟicolmuṉ yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭi vakaram keṭutalum - palari aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭiyil vakaram keṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟu aṟi colmuṉ yātu eṉ viṉā iṭi oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varum yātu eṉṉum viṉāmoḻiyiṭi uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - iraṇṭum marūukkaḷatu muṭipiṉiṭattup payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
'oṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, vakara uyirmey eṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāreṉpatum yāvateṉpatum nilimoḻiyāyp piṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar' eṉavum, "yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum" (kuṟun. 78) eṉavum varum.
'payiṉṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, palaraṟi collum oṉṟaṟi collum varumoḻiyāya vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu: yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa varum.	(30)
 
tokimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

6
urupiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, urupukaḷoṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, urupiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl tokuttup puṇartta ceyki īṇṭu niṉṟum virittup puṇark kiṉṟārākaliṉ, tokimarapiṉōṭu iyipu uṭittāyiṟṟu.
a, ā - mutaliya īṟukaḷ āṟum urupiṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu
174.	aā uū ēau eṉṉum
	appā lāṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi urupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyi.
it talic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akara ākāra ukara ūkāra ēkāra aukāra īṟukaḷ urupiṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar - a ā u ū ē au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa ak kūṟṟu āṟaṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi urupiṟku cāriyi iṉ - vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ varumoḻiyāy vanta nilimikku iṭivarum cāriyi iṉcāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉi, viḷaviṉoṭu, viḷaviṟku, viḷaviṉ, viḷaviṉatu, viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum; palāviṉi, palāvi ṉoṭu eṉavum; kaṭuviṉi, kaṭuviṉoṭu eṉavum; kaḻūuviṉi, kaḻūuviṉoṭu eṉavum; cēviṉi, cēvi ṉoṭu eṉavum; vuviṉi vuviṉoṭu eṉavum karuvi aṟintu oṭṭuka.	(1)
akara īṟṟuc cila peyarkaḷ vaṟṟoṭu poruntutal
175.	pallavi nutaliya akara iṟupeyar
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal ecca miṉṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya īṟṟuḷ akarīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pallavi nutaliya peyar iṟu akaram - paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccam iṉṟu - vaṟṟuccāriyiyoṭu poruntutali oḻital illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pallavaṟṟi, pallavaṟṟoṭu; uḷḷavaṟṟi, uḷḷavaṟ ṟoṭu; illavaṟṟi, illavaṟṟoṭu; cillavaṟṟi, cillavaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'ecca miṉṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟṟē muṭiyumeṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēl iṉpeṟṟaṉa piṟacāriyiyum peṟumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	makatti, nilātti eṉa varum.	(2)
ṉayāṉa eṉ viṉāvum aṟṟātal
176.	yāveṉ viṉāvum āyiyal tiriyātu.
iḵtu, ākāra vīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāvum a iyal tiriyātu - yā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāp peyarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa vaṟṟup peṟum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(3)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
177.	cuṭṭumuta lukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi
	oṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukarīṟṟuc col aṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaram keṭum - tāṉ poruntiya meyyi oḻittu ukaram keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉi, ataṉoṭu; itaṉi, itaṉoṭu; utaṉi, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(4)
cuṭṭumutal aikāram vaṟṟuppeṟṟup puṇarumāṟu
178.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya aiyeṉ iṟuti
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittē.
iḵtu, aikāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku muṭipukūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuc col, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu - vaṟṟuccāriyiyoṭu porunti av vīṟṟu aikāram niṟṟalu murittu; nillāmiyu murittu. e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟi, aviyaṟṟoṭu; iviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟ ṟoṭu; uviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
aikāram keṭṭavaḻi, niṉṟa vakarattiṉi vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu avaṟṟi, ivaṟṟi, uvaṟṟi eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu, im muṭipu cuṭṭumutal vakara vīṟṟōṭu ottamiyiṉ, īṇṭu itu kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟalām piṟa eṉiṉ, aḵtu okkum; ivvāṟu kūṟuvaṉa mēlum uḷa; avaṟṟiṟkellām āciriyaṉ karuttu aṟintu koḷḷappaṭu meṉpatu.	(5)
ṉayāṉa viṉāviṉ aikāra iṟutiyum vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarumāṟu
179.	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	āyiyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar
	āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuc collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - mēṟkūṟiya cuṭṭumutal aikāram pōla vaṟṟup peṟum iyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; a vayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭum keṭum - avviṭattu vakaram aikārattoṭukūṭak keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
vakaram vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟeṉṟu (134) keṭuvataṉi, īṇṭuk kēṭōtiyavataṉāṉ piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟuppeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kariyavaṟṟi, ceyyavaṟṟi eṉa varum.	(6)
ṉanīṉa niṉ ātal
180.	nīyeṉ orupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum
	āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟā kummē.
iḵtu, īkāravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar neṭumutal kuṟukum - nī eṉṉum oru peyar neṭitākiya mutal kuṟukum; a vayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum - avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	niṉṉi, niṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'orupeyar' eṉṟatu, niṉ eṉpatum vēṟoru peyar pōṟali vilakkiṟṟu. 'peyarkuṟukum' eṉṉātu 'mutalkuṟukum' eṉṟatu, ap peyariṉ neṭṭeḻuttu niliyatu ak kuṟukka meṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. 'neṭumutal' eṉṟatu am moḻimutaliṉ nakaram kuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. īṇṭu, uyirmey yoṟṟumipaṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu.
cāriyippēṟṟiṭi eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyatu, mūṉṟām urupiṉ kaṇ cāriyi peṟṟē vanta atikāram māṟṟi niṉṟatu.	(7)
ōkāriṟuti oṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupoṭu puṇartal
181.	ōkāra iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu, ōkāra īṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟutikkuc cāriyi oṉ - ōkāravīṟṟukku iṭivarum cāriyi oṉcāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōoṉi, kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(8)
aā īṟṟu marappeyar ēḻaṉurupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
182.	aā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavik(ku)
	attoṭuñ civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupē.
iḵtu, akara ākāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku, urupiṉkaṇ eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku - a ā eṉṟu collappaṭum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarākiya coṟku, attoṭum civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupu - muṉkūṟiya iṉṉōṭaṉṟi (174) attōṭum poruntum ēḻām urupu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(9)
ñakara nakarap puḷḷiyiṟuti iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal
183. ñaneṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi.
itu, puḷḷiyīṟṟu ñakāravīṟum nakāravīṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi - ña na eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭku varum cāriyi iṉcāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uri"ṉi, uri"ṉoṭu; poruniṉi, poruniṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(10)
cuṭṭumutal vakarīṟu mūṉṟum vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭital
184.	cuṭṭumutal vakaram aiyum meyyum
	keṭṭa iṟuti yiyaltiri piṉṟē.
itu, vakaravīṟu nāṉkaṉuḷḷum cuṭṭumutal vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal vakaram - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakaravīṟṟuccol, aiyum meyyum keṭṭa iṟuti iyaltiripu iṉṟu - aikāramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyum keṭṭu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭinta cuṭṭumutal aikāravīṟṟiyalpiṉ tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟi, avaṟṟoṭu; ivaṟṟi, ivaṟṟoṭu; uvaṟṟi, uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(11)
ēṉi vakarīṟṟu muṭipu
185.	ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu, vakaravīṟṟuḷ oḻinta vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - oḻinta vakara vīṟu iṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tevviṉi, tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu itu uriccol laṉṟōveṉiṉ, uriccollē yeṉiṉum paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka.	(12)
makara īṟṟuccol muṉṉar attu varutal
186.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attē cāriyi.
itu, makarīṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attu cāriyi - makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum cāriyi attuc cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maratti, marattoṭu eṉa varum.	(13)
avvīṟṟuc cila peyar iṉ peṟṟu muṭital
187.	iṉṉiṭi varūum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, makaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ iṭivarum moḻiyum uḷa - makaravīṟu attuccāriyi yoḻiya iṉcāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	urumiṉi, urumiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(14)
ṉanumṉa īṟucāriyi peṟātu urupoṭu muṭital
188.	nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟki yākum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟuti iyaṟki ākum - num eṉṉum makaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nummi, nummoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(15)
tām, nām, yām - urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
189.	tāmnām eṉṉu makara iṟutiyum
	yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉō raṉṉa
	āe ākum yāmeṉ iṟuti
	āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum
	ēṉi yiraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ muṉkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
