(i-ḷ.) tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yām eṉṉum iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṉṉa - tām nām eṉṟu collappaṭum makaravīṟum yām eṉṉum makaravīṟum mēṟkūṟiya num eṉṉum makaravīṟupōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitaliyuṭiyavām; yām eṉ iṟuti ā e ākum - yām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu moḻi ākāram ekāramām; a vayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu yakaramākiya mey keṭutal vēṇṭum; ēṉi iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum - oḻinta iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutal kuṟukiniṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tammi, tammoṭu; nammi, nammoṭu; emmi, emmoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'āvayiṉ mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taṅkaṇ, naṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa varum.	(16)
ṉellāmṉa urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
190.	ellā meṉṉum iṟuti muṉṉar
	vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellām eṉṉum iṟutimuṉṉar - ellām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc colliṉmuṉpu, vaṟṟu eṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya attum (186) iṉṉumiṉṟi (187) vaṟṟu eṉṉum cāriyi muṭiyattōṉṟi muṭiyum, ummi niliyum iṟutiyāṉa - um eṉṉum cāriyi nilipeṟum iṟutikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellāvaṟṟoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka. 
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkām urupiṉkaṇṇum āṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu koḷka.	(17)
ṉellāmṉa uyartiṇiyāyiṉ muṭiyumāṟu
191. uyartiṇi yāyiṉ nammiṭi varumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇiyāyiṉ nam iṭivarum - ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar aḵṟiṇip peyaraṉṟi uyartiṇip peyarāy niṟkumāyiṉ, nam iṭivarum - nam eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum e-ṟu.
nilimoḻiyoṟṟum mēl 'vaṟṟumici yoṟṟu' eṉṟu keṭṭuniṉṟa atikārattāṟ keṭukka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē ummup peṟutalum, ataṉkaṇ ukaram cila urupiṉkaṇ keṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellānammiyum, ellānammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.	(18)
ṉellārumṉa, ṉellīrumṉa eṉpaṉa urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
192.	ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum
	ellīru meṉṉu muṉṉili yiṟutiyum
	oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭumeṉa moḻipa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
	ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa
	tammiṭi varūum paṭarkki mēṉa
	nummiṭi varūum muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu, makara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkki iṟutiyum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉili iṟutiyum - ellārum eṉṟu collappaṭum paṭarkkiyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum ellīrum eṉṟu collap paṭum muṉṉiliyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum, oṟṟum ukaramum keṭum eṉa moḻipa - ivaṟṟiṉ makaravoṟṟum ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa ukaramum keṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar, rakarappuḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - av vukaram keṭuvaḻi ataṉāl ūrappaṭṭa rakarappuḷḷi keṭātu niṟṟal vēṇṭum, iṟutiyāṉa ummi niliyum - av viru moḻikkum iṟutikkaṇ um eṉṉum cāriyi nilipeṟum, paṭarkki mēṉa tam iṭi varum - paṭarkkiyiṭattut tammuc cāriyi iṭi varum, muṉṉili moḻikku num iṭivarum - muṉṉili moḻikku nummuccāriyi iṭivarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ellārtammiyum, ellār tammoṭum; ellīr nummiyum, ellīr nummoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
'paṭarkki', 'muṉṉili' eṉṟa mikutiyāṉ, makaravīṟṟut taṉmip peyariṭikkaṇ nammuccāriyiyum, īṟṟukkaṇ ummuc cāriyiyum peṟṟumuṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kariyēnammiyum, kariyēnammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
paṭarkkippeyar muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, rakāravīṟṟup paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilippeyarum iṟutikkaṇ ummoṭu tammuc cāriyiyum nummuccāriyiyum (iṭiyē) peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kariyārtammiyum, cāṉṟīrnummiyum eṉa oṭṭuka.
ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, am mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇ (eḻut. 190) ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu eṭuttōtiyavaṟṟiṟkum ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭavaṟṟiṟkum koḷka.	(19)
tāṉ, yāṉ - urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
193.	tāṉyāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum
	mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu, ṉakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum a īr iṟutiyum - tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭum avviraṇṭu ṉakara vīṟum, mēl muppeyaroṭum vēṟupāṭu ila  mēl makaravīṟṟuṭ collappaṭṭa (189) mūṉṟu peyaroṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukiyum yāṉ eṉpataṉkaṇ ākāram ekāramāy yakaraṅkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	taṉṉi, taṉṉoṭu; eṉṉi, eṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.(20)
aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ - urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
194.	aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyiru moḻikkum
	attum iṉṉum uṟaḻat tōṉṟal
	otta teṉpa uṇaru mōrē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ puḻaṉ a iru moḻikkum - aḻaṉ puḻaṉākiya av viru moḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻat tōṉṟal ottatu eṉpa uṇarumōr - attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum māṟivarat tōṉṟu talip poruntiṟṟeṉpa uṇaruvōr e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḻatti, aḻattoṭu; aḻaṉiṉi, aḻaṉiṉoṭu; puḻatti, puḻattoṭu; puḻaṉiṉi, puḻaṉiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'tōṉṟal' eṉṟataṉāṉ, evaṉ eṉṟum, eṉ eṉṟum niṟutti, vaṟṟukkoṭuttu vēṇṭum ceykiceytu evaṟṟi, evaṟṟoṭu eṉavum, eṟṟi, eṟṟoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
'ottatu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti, attum iṉṉum koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu ekiṉatti, ekiṉattoṭu; ekiṉiṉi, ekiṉiṉoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
attu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, attup peṟṟavaḻi iṉitu icikku meṉak koḷka.	(21)
ṉēḻṉa aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal
195.	aṉṉeṉ cāriyi ēḻaṉ iṟuti
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkit teṉpa.
itu, ḻakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉ eṉ cāriyi ēḻaṉ iṟuti muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkittu eṉpa - aṉ eṉṉum cāriyi ēḻeṉṉum colliṟutiyiṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟum iyalpiṉiyuṭitteṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, piṟavum aṉ peṟuvaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pūḻaṉi, pūḻaṉoṭu; yāḻaṉi, yāḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(22)
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟuti iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal
196.	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
	muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutimuṉṉar muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyi - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	varakiṉi, varakiṉoṭu; nākiṉi, nākiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa cāriyi peṟuvaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ, kariyataṉi, kariyataṉoṭu eṉa varum.	(23)
avaṟṟuṭcila ṭa ṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutal
197.	neṭṭeḻut timpar oṟṟumikat tōṉṟum
	appāṉ moḻikaḷ alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu impar oṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉāka (iṭi) iṉavoṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum, appāl moḻikaḷ avvaḻiyāṉa - ak kūṟṟumoḻikaḷ allāta iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē e-ṟu.
av vīṟu iṉcāriyi peṟuvatu; āṇṭāyiṉ peṟātu.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu, yāṟṟi, yāṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.
appāṉ moḻikaḷāvaṉa ka ca ta pa kkaḷ. ivi iṉavoṟṟu mikāveṉṟu koḷka.
'tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirttoṭar moḻiyum iṉpeṟātu iṉavoṟṟu mikutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pakaṭṭi, pakaṭṭoṭu; muyiṟṟi, muyiṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(24)
āṇṭu avi iṉcāriyi peṟāmi
198.	avitām
	iyaṟkiya vākuñ ceyaṟkiya eṉpa.
itu, mēṟ cāriyi vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟ cāriyi peṟāveṉa vilakkappaṭṭa avitām, iyaṟkiya ākum ceyaṟkiya eṉpa - iyalpāy muṭitali yuṭiyavākum ceytiyiyuṭiya veṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'ceyaṟkiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉavoṟṟu mikkaṉa ciṟupāṉmi iṉpeṟutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭiṉi, yāṭṭiṉoṭu; muyiṟṟiṉi, muyiṟṟiṉoṭu eṉa varum.	(25)
eṇṇuppeyaratu kuṟṟukarīṟu aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal
199.	eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara vīṟu aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉṟaṉi, oṉṟaṉoṭu; iraṇṭaṉi, iraṇṭaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(26)
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa orupāṉ mutaliya ātal
200.	oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūum
	ellā eṇṇuñ colluṅ kāli
	āṉiṭi variṉum māṉa milli
	aḵteṉ kiḷavi āvayiṟ keṭumē
	uytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākap pattu ūrntu varum ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar mutalākap pattu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarāṉ ūrappaṭṭu varum ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, colluṅ kāli - muṭipu colluṅkālattu, āṉ iṭivariṉum māṉam illi - av vīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya aṉṉēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyi iṭivariṉum kuṟṟamilli, a vayiṉ aḵtu eṉ kiḷavi keṭum - av vāṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi aḵtu eṉṉum col keṭum, paḵkāṉmey uytal vēṇṭum - atu keṭuvaḻi av vakarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pakaramākiya mey keṭātu niṟṟali vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupāṉi, orupāṉoṭu; orupaḵtaṉi, orupaḵtaṉoṭu; irupaḵtaṉi, irupaḵtaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
'colluṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum āṉ peṟṟu, avvīṟṟiṉ atu eṉṉum coṟkeṭṭu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpāṉi, oṉpāṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(27)
yātu, āytam iṭivanta cuṭṭumutal ukaram
ivi aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal
201.	yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta iṟutiyum aṉṉoṭu civaṇum
	āytaṅ keṭutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum, ak kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyum - yātu eṉa varum kuṟṟukara īṟum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - aṉcāriyi yoṭu poruntum, ā vayiṉāṉa āytam keṭutal - avviṭattu āytam keṭuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yātaṉi, yātaṉoṭu; ataṉi, ataṉoṭu; itaṉi, itaṉoṭu; utaṉi, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(28)
ticippeyar ēḻaṉurupoṭu puṇarumāṟu
202.	ēḻaṉ urupiṟkut ticippeyar muṉṉarc
	cāriyik kiḷavi iyaṟkiyu mākum
	āvayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉ urupoṭu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻaṉ urupiṟku - ēḻām vēṟṟumikku, ticip peyar muṉṉar - ticiyi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, cāriyikkiḷavi iyaṟkiyum ākum - iv vīṟṟiṟku muṉkūṟiya iṉcāriyiyākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; a vayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭum keṭum - iyalpāyavaḻip peyar iṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭum keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, kiḻakkiṉkaṇ, teṟkiṉkaṇ, mēṟkiṉkaṇ eṉavum; vaṭakkaṇ, kiḻakkaṇ, teṟkaṇ, mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum.
urupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, kīḻcār, kīḻpuṭi; mēlcār, mēlpuṭi; teṉcār, teṉpuṭi; vaṭacār, vaṭapuṭi eṉa ivvāṟu cāriyi peṟātu tirintu muṭivaṉavellām koḷka.	(29)
ivviyaṟkup puṟaṉaṭi
203.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	colliya alla ēṉiya vellām
	tēruṅ kāli urupoṭu civaṇic
	cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.
iḵtu, iv vōttiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum colliya alla ēṉiya ellām - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collu meṉa muṭipu colliyavi allāta oḻintavi yellām, tērum kāli - ārāyuṅkālattu, urupoṭu civaṇic cāriyi niliyum kaṭappāṭu ila - urupukaḷoṭu porunti iṉ cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟimiyi yuṭiya valla (niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum) e-ṟu.
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa aintu; uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu oṉṟu. iviyum eṭuttōtiya īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉavumellām īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇiṉi, maṇṇi; vēyiṉi, vēyi; nāriṉi, nāri; kalliṉi, kalli;  muḷḷiṉi, muḷḷi   eṉavum; kiḷiyiṉi, kiḷiyi eṉavum; poṉṉiṉi, poṉṉi   eṉavum; tāḻiṉi, tāḻi; tīyiṉi, tīyi; kaḻiyiṉi, kaḻiyi eṉavum  oṭṭuka.
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa palavākaliṉ, atu muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. 'tēruṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupukaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṉṟu tam poruḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi muṭiyum muṭipuvēṟṟumi yellām koḷka.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇiṉik koṇarntāṉ, nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ; maliyoṭu porutatu mālyāṉi; marattāṟ puṭittāṉ; marattukkup pōṉāṉ; kākkiyiṟ karitu; kākkiyatu pali; marattukkaṇ kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu urupiyalākaliṉ, 'urupoṭu civaṇi' eṉa vēṇṭā vaṉṟē, ataṉāṉ, urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ceṉṟa cāriyikaḷellām īṟṟup potumuṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum cellu meṉpatu koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, uyirīṟum meyyīṟum cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu: nampiyi, koṟṟaṉi eṉa varum.	(30)

urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

7
uyirmayaṅkiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, uyirīṟu vaṉ kaṇattoṭum ciṟupāṉmi piṟa kaṇattoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttaliṉ uyirmayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl urupu puṇarcci kūṟi īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, mēlata ṉōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.
akarīṟṟuppeyarkku vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi alvaḻimuṭipu
204.	akara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
	tattam otta oṟṟiṭi mikumē.
it taliccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akaravīṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akara iṟuti peyarnili muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi ka ca ta pa tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻikkaṇ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, tam tam otta oṟṟu iṭimikum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa ak ka ca ta pakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷakkuṟitu, makakkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
'otta' oṟṟeṉṉātu 'tattam' eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaravīṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, iṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, akaram taṉṉi uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taṭakki, tavakkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivi uriccol valleḻuttuppēṟu. taṭañcevi, taṭantōḷ eṉa ivi melleḻuttuppēṟu. 
	"maṭava maṉṟa taṭavu nilik koṉṟi" (kuṟun. 66) eṉpatu iṭiccol muṭipu. 
	akkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉpatu taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikuti.
	avyātu eṉpataṟku iṭiyeḻuttu mikuti. av vaḻakitu eṉpatu uyirkkaṇattu muṭipu.	(1)
avvīṟṟu viṉiyeccamum iṭiccollum
mēlimuṭipu peṟutal
205.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	eṉaveṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum
	āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akarīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭipum, iṭiccol muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum - akara vīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum uvamaccollum, eṉa eṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - eṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa eccac collum cuṭṭākiya akaravīṟum, āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum - āṅka eṉṟu collappaṭum uriyaciyākiya collum, ñāṅkar kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - mēlic cūttirattuc coṉṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇakkoṇṭāṉ, tiṉakkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa viṉiyeccam. 
	mēlaṉavellām iṭiccol. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ eṉpatu uvamam. 
	koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu eṉa eṉ eccam.
	akkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭiṉ iṟuti. 
	āṅkak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu uriyacik kiḷavi.	 (2)
akaraccuṭṭu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
206.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ
	oṭṭiya oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, akaraccuṭṭu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ña na ma tōṉṟiṉ - akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaḷ mutalākiya moḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭimikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇṇē mikutali vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aññālam, nūl, maṇi eṉa varum.	(3)
accuṭṭu iṭikkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
207.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakaram oṟṟum.
itu, cuṭṭu iṭikkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ya va muṉ variṉ vakaram oṟṟum - yakara vakaram mutal moḻiyākac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ iṭikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avyāḻ, avvaḷi eṉa varum.	(4)
uyirkkaṇam varuvaḻiyum aḵtu ammuṭipu peṟutal
208. uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
iḵtu, accuṭṭu uyirkkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum a iyal tiriyātu - uyir mutal moḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar variṉum mēṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku muṭiyum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avvaṭi, avvāṇṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
iṭimikka vakarattiṉi 'neṟiyiyal'(161) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nilimoḻit toḻileṉpatuvē kūṟappaṭṭatu. 'tiriyātu' eṉṟataṉāṉē, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
accuṭṭuc ceyyuṭkaṇ nīṭal
209.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu, accuṭṭuc ceyyuḷuḷ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akarac cuṭṭu nīḷumpaṭiyāka varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	"āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē" (kiḷavi. 1) eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, im muṭipu vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇamellāvaṟṟoṭum ceṉṟatu. utāraṇam peṟṟavaḻik koḷka.	(6)
ṉacāvaṉa vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi iṟuti vakaram keṭutal
210.	cāva eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ eccattu
	iṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
itu, mēl 'viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum' (uyir. 2) eṉṟa muṭipiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cāva eṉṉum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram keṭutalum urittu - cāva eṉṟu collappaṭum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakara voṟṟum keṭātu niṟṟalē yaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum uriya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cākkuttiṉāṉ, cīṟiṉāṉ, takarttāṉ, puṭittāṉ  eṉa varum.
itaṉi 'viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum' (uyir. 2) eṉṟataṉpiṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum an nilimoḻik kēṭu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cāñāṉṟār eṉa varum.	(7)
akara īṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭiyum coṟkaḷ
211.	aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum
	ceymmaṉa eṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
	ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
	ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
	ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	amma eṉṉum uripporuṭ kiḷaviyum
	palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟuḷ orucārp peyarkkum viṉikkum iṭikkum muṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyum, eytātatu eytuvittum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. aṉṉaveṉpatum ceyyiyaveṉpatum palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi yeṉpatum eytiyatu vilakkiṉa. maṟṟiyaṉa eytātatu eytuvittaṉa.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamakkiḷaviyum - aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvama urupākiya iṭiccollum, aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum - aṇiyārik karutiya viḷiyākiya nilimiyi yuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarccollum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻil iṟu collum - ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiccollākiya akaravīṟṟuc collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ kiḷaviyum - ēvalik karutiya viyaṅkōḷākiya viṉiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya viṉiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉi eñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyiya eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyeccamākiya viṉic collum, amma eṉṉum uripporuḷ kiḷaviyum - amma eṉṟu collappaṭum uri yacip poruṇmiyiyuṭiya iṭiccollum, palavaṟṟu iṟutip peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa aṉṟi aṉittum - paṉmip poruḷkaḷiṉ akara vīṟṟup palaveṉṉum peyarccollumākiya avvaṉittuc collum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	poṉ aṉṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum;  ūra koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum; uṇmaṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; celka kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; uṇṭa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; itaṉ etirmaṟi uṇṇāta kutiri eṉavum; itaṉ kuṟippu nalla kutiri, cennāy eṉavum; uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum;  pala kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum varum.
'ūra koḷ' eṉpatu 'uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyar' (154) eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ, ūraṉ eṉpatu ūra eṉa īṟu tirintamiyiṉ āṇṭu aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
viḷinilik kiḷaviyākiya peyari muṉvittataṉāṉ, ceyta veṉpataṉ maṟiyaṉṟic ceyyum eṉpataṟku maṟiyākiya ceyyāta eṉpatum av viyalpu uṭitteṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vārāta koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.
'uriyiṟkōṭal' eṉpataṉāṉ, viyaṅkōḷ muṉ vikkaṟ pālataṉi muṉviyātu ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉi muṉvittataṉāṉ, iv viyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymmaṉa eṉpatu ciṟappuṭitteṉap peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṉ, 'ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷum' iv viyalpu muṭipu uṭitteṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	"maṉṉiya perumanī" (puṟam. 6) eṉa varum.
'palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi' eṉpataṉi amma eṉpataṟku muṉ viyātataṉāṉ, pala eṉpatu mēṟkūṟum ceyyuḷ muṭipiṉ (214) tirintu muṭital īṇṭi iyalpiṟ ciṟappiṉ ṟeṉpatūum, akara vīṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟāta muṟṟuviṉiyum viṉikkuṟippum iv viyalpiṉa veṉpatūum peṟappaṭum.
uṇṭaṉa kutiri eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉi. kariya kutiri eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉik kuṟippu.
'taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, pala eṉpataṉ iṉamākiya cila eṉpataṟkum av viyalpu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cila kutiri eṉavarum.	(8)
ṉavāḻiyaṉa iṟuti yakaram keṭutal
212.	vāḻiya veṉṉuñ cēyaveṉ kiḷavi
	iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
iḵtu ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷkaḷiṉ oṉṟaṟku eytiya iyalpu vilakki vikāram kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vāḻiya eṉṉum cēya eṉ kiḷavi - vāḻiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa, av vāḻuṅkālam aṇmiyavaṉṟic cēymiya eṉṟu uṇarttum col, iṟuti yakaram keṭutalum urittu - taṉ ṉiṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟum keṭātu muṭitalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vāḻikoṟṟā eṉa varum.
'cēyāṉ kiḷavi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, 
am muṭipu iv 'vāḻiya' eṉpataṟku ēval kaṇṇāta niliya teṉpatu viḷakkiya niṉṟatu. 'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vāḻiñeḷḷā eṉa varum.	(9)
‘amma’ iṟuti akaram nīṭalum urittātal
213.	uripporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār.
iḵtu, amma eṉpataṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uripporuḷ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār - uriyacip poruṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya amma eṉṉum iṭiccol taṉ īṟṟakaram akaramāy niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭu muṭitaliyum variyār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ammākoṟṟā eṉa varum.
'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, an nīṭci iyalpukaṇattum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ammāñeḷḷā eṉa varum.	(10)
‘palāam cilāam’ eṉṉum muṭipu
214.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi uḷavē
	ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.
itu, mēṟ "palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi" (uyirmayaṅkiyal 8) eṉṟu ōtiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa - pala eṉṉum colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; (yāṇṭu uḷaveṉiṉ), ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa - ceyyuḷātalik karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum ceyyuḷ muṭipuṭiya moḻikaḷiṉkaṇṇē e-ṟu.
'ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya moḻi' eṉṉātu 'toṭarmoḻi' eṉṟata ṉāṉ, ip palaveṉpatu nīḷumvaḻi varumoḻiyāvatu cilaveṉpatē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
'ceyyuḷāṉa' eṉṉātu 'ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pala eṉṉum moḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi nilimoḻi akarap pēṟum varumoḻi ñakāra melleḻuttup pēṟum, varumoḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka.
'uṇṭu' eṉṉātu 'uḷa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cilaveṉṉum varumoḻi yiṟuti nīṭalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'palāañ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar' eṉavarum. itaṉ colnili palacila eṉṉum cevveṇ.	(11)
pala, cila - tammuṉ tām vantu puṇartal
215.	toṭaral iṟutit tammuṉ tāmvariṉ
	lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟātalu murittē.
itu, pala cila eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟit tiripum uṇṭeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toṭaral iṟutit tammuṉ tāmvariṉ - toṭar moḻi yallāta īreḻut torumoḻiyākiya pala cila eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col tammuṉṉē tāmvariṉ, lakaram ṟakara oṟṟu ātalum urittu - tam īṟṟil niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟu ṟakaravoṟṟāyt tirintu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṟpala koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ciṟcila vitti eṉavum varum.
'taṉmuṉ tāṉ' eṉa orumiyāṟ kūṟātu 'tammuṉ tāmvariṉ' eṉṟa paṉmiyāṉ, mēṟ pala cila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭum taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa. 'toṭara liṟuti tammuṉ variṉ' eṉṉātu 'tām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, im muṭipiṉkaṇ palaveṉpataṉmuṉ pala varuka, cilaveṉpataṉ muṉ cila varuka eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
'lakaram ṟakaramām' eṉṉātu 'lakaram ṟakara voṟṟām' eṉṟa ñāpakattāṉ, akaram keṭumeṉṟārākak koḷka. aruttāpatti mukattāl, tammuṉ tāmvariṉ lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟā meṉavē, tammuṉ piṟa vantaviṭattu lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākātu akaram keṭumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	'palpaṭi' (poruḷ. 72), palyāṉi, cilpaṭi, cilvēḷvi eṉa varum.
'toṭara liṟuti' eṉpatu cuṭṭallatu ōreḻut torumoḻi akaramiṉmiyiṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻimēṟ cellātāyiṟṟu. uritteṉṟatu, akaravīṟṟorumi paṟṟi.	(12)
appuṇarcciyiṭi valleḻuttu vikaṟpamātal
216.	valleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
ituvum, mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum iṉṉum oru muṭipu vēṟṟumi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) val eḻuttu iyaṟki uṟaḻattōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya pala cila eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akaravīṟṟup potuvitiyiṟ (204) kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikutiyatu iyalpu, mikutalum mikāmiyumāki uṟaḻntuvarat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palappala, cilaccila; palapala, cilacila eṉa varum.
īṇṭum tammuṉ tām varutal koḷka.
'iyaṟki' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṉkūṟiya paṟpala, ciṟcila eṉṉum muṭipōṭu palpala, cilcila eṉṉum muṭipupeṟṟu uṟaḻcciyātal koḷka.
'tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaram keṭa lakaram āytamum mel leḻuttumāyt tirintu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paḵṟāṉi, paṉmaram; ciḵṟāḻici, ciṉṉūl eṉa varum.	(13)
akara īṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi vēṟṟumi muṭipu
217.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, akara īṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattoṭu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - akara vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum mēṟkūṟiya (204) alvaḻiyōṭu oru taṉmittāyk ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi tattam oṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum. viḷakkuṟumi eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumik kaṇṇum koḷka.	(14)
avvīṟṟu marappeyar muṭipu
218.	marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟu marappeyark kiḷavikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - akaravīṟṟu marappeyarākiya col melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu, viḷañcetiḷ; tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(15)
‘maka’ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
219.	makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉcāriyi - akaravīṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyarccolliṟku vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varum cāriyi iṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makaviṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟu variyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattuk kaṇṇum cellumeṉpatākaliṉ, makaviṉñāṇ eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum koḷka.	(16)
aḵtu attuccāriyiyum peṟutal
220.	attavaṇ variṉum varinili iṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) attu avaṇ variṉum vari nili iṉṟu - mēṟkūṟiya iṉṉēyaṉṟi attu eṉṉum cāriyi iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu am maka eṉṉum colliṭattu vantu muṭiyiṉum nīkkum nilimi iṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makattukki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉac ceyki aṟintu muṭikka.
'avaṇ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum, makaviṉki eṉa mēl iṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ vantavaḻi viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka.
'nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, makampālyāṭu eṉa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. 	(17)
‘pala’ vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟutal
221.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - pala eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col urupupuṇarccikkaṇ (175) vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarnta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palavaṟṟukkōṭu, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(18)
ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
222.	ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ākāra vīṟṟup peyar (al vaḻikkaṇ) akara vīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tārāk kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(19)
‘ceyyā’ eṉṉum viṉiyeccamum aṟṟātal
223.	ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyā eṉṉum viṉi eñcu kiḷaviyum - (peyarē yaṉṟi) ceyyā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉiyeccac collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum avviyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇāk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
'tiriyātu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, 'ceyyā' eṉṉum peyareccamum avvāṟu muṭiyumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇāk koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.	(20)
ākāra īṟṟu nilimoḻi ummittoki muṭipu
224.	ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi
	meymmi yāka akaram mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummittoki muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi - ummi tokka irupeyarākiya tokiccol, meymmiyāka akaram mikum - meyyāka nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uvāap patiṉāṉku.
'meymmi yāka' eṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttuk koṭukka.
'ummi tokka' eṉṉātu 'eñciya' eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumi yāṉ, im muṭipu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokikkum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	arāap pāmpu eṉavarum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉ, eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum peyareccattiṟkum akarappēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	irāak koṭitu; irāak kākki eṉavarum.
nilimoḻi eḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumākaliṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum.	(21)
ākārīṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭiyum coṟkaḷ
225.	āvu māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
	yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
	ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum
	taṉtoḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu
	aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku iyalpukūṟi eytiyatu vilakkutalum, eytātatu eytuvittalum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āvum māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum - ā eṉṉum peyarccollum mā eṉṉum peyarccollum viḷittali yuṭiya peyarākiya uyartiṇiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāvum palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāppeyarum aḵṟiṇip paṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum ākāravīṟṟu muṟṟuviṉic collum, ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum - muṉṉiliyil ēval viṉiccollik kuṟittuvarum uriyaciyākiya miyā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu iṭic collum, taṉtoḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu aṉṟi aṉittum - taṉatu toḻiliṉic collum ākāra viṉāviṉiyuṭiya viṉic collumākiya avvaṉittum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum;  mā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; ūrā koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum;  yā kuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉavum; uṇṇā kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; kēṇmiyā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
'viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum', 'palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum', 'ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum', 'taṉtoḻi lurikkum viṉāvum' eytātatu eytuvikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ūrā koḷ eṉpatu 'uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyareṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, muṉ (uyir. 8) kūṟiṟṟē kūṟuka.	(22)
ākārīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
226.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - (ākāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum akaravīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tārākkāl, ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉavarum.	(23)
nilimoḻikkaṇ akara eḻuttuppēṟu tōṉṟum iṭam
227.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟiṟkum ōreḻut torumoḻi ākāravīṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyat tōṉṟum - (nilimoḻikkaṇ) akaramākiya eḻuttu aṟiyat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palāakkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; kāakkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
ōreḻut torumoḻi muṟkūṟātavataṉāṉ, ataṉkaṇ akarappēṟu ciṟupāṉmi yeṉak koḷka.
'aṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, av viruvaḻiyum akaram poruntiya vaḻiyē varutalum, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, ivvīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟa vaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, iv vuyirīṟṟil varum urupīṟṟuc ceykiyum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aṇṇāattuk kuḷam, uvāattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, uvāattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, yāvaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa ivi piṟamuṭipu. mūṅkāviṉtōl eṉpatu valleḻuttu vīḻvu. iṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu urupīṟṟuc ceyki.	(24)
‘irā’ vēṟṟumimuṭipil akaram peṟāmi
228.	irāveṉ kiḷavik kakara milli.
iḵtu, av akarappēṟṟiṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) irā eṉ kiḷavikku akaram illi - irā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟuc colliṟku muṉkūṟiya akarappēṟu illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	irākkoṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(25)
‘nilā’ attuccāriyi peṟutal
229.	nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu oruvaḻi akaram vilakki attuvakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nilā eṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum - nilā eṉṉum col attuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nilimoḻit toḻili nilimoḻit toḻil vilakku mākaliṉ, attu vakuppa akaram vīḻntatu.	(26)
melleḻuttu mikum ivvīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ
230.	yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
	āmup peyarum melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvum taḷāvum a muppeyarum - yā eṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa collum piṭā eṉṉum collum taḷā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, melleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāaṅkōṭu, piṭāaṅkōṭu, taḷāaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
melleḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻit toḻilātaliṉ, varumoḻi valleḻutti vilakkiṟṟu.	(27)
avi valleḻuttu mikutalu muṭimi
231.	valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmi mikku muṭiyum eṉa iṟantatu kāttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarum melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāakkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
'māṉa milli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	yāaviṉkōṭu, piṭāaviṉkōṭu, taḷāaviṉ kōṭu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāattukkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi attuppē ṟuṇṭēṉum koḷka. av vakarappēṟṟōṭu val leḻuttup peṟutaliṉ. 'yāmarak kiḷavi' eṉpataṉik 'kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum' eṉpataṉpiṉ viyāta itaṉāṉ, irāviṟ koṇṭāṉ, nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉpataṟku nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu īṟṟup potu muṭipāyiṉa vāṟu aṟika.	(28)
‘mā’ marappeyarum ṉāṉavum ṉamāṉavum (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) muṭiyumāṟu
232.	māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum
	āmup peyarum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	akaram valleḻut taviyavaṇ niliyā
	ṉakaram oṟṟum āvum māvum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māmarakkiḷaviyum āvum māvum a muppeyarum avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - māmaramākiya collum ā eṉṉum collum mā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarccollum mēṟkūṟiya (230) yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṉoṭum oru taṉmiyavāy valleḻuttup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalumuṭiya, āvum māvum akaram valleḻuttu avi avaṇ niliyā ṉakaram oṟṟum - avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum muṉpeṟṟu niṉṟa akaramum (227) valleḻuttumākiya avi avviṭattu nilipeṟāvāy ṉakaram oṟṟākap peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	māaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
'aṟiya' (uyir. 24) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmi māṅkōṭu eṉa akaramiṉṟiyum varum.
iṉi 'avaṇ' eṉṟataṉāṉ kāyāṅkōṭu, āṇāṅkōṭu, nuṇāṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōlap piṟavum melleḻuttup peṟutalum; aṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, iṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, uṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, eṅkāk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttup peṟutalum; āviṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉpeṟutalum, peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka.
'avaṟṟō raṉṉa' eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉ peṟṟuniṉṟatu mel leḻuttākaliṉ, 'akaram valleḻut taviyava ṇiliyā' eṉṟu ōtaṟ pālataṉṟeṉiṉ, mēl "ūveṉ ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum" (uyir. 67) eṉṟavaḻi, am māṭṭēṟṟāṉē ataṉ valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷal vēṇṭit 'tirintataṉṟiripatu' eṉṉum nayattāṉē mel leḻutti valleḻuttāka ōtiṉāreṉak koḷka.	(29)
‘ā’ eṉum peyarkku ṉakaroṟṟōṭu akaraccāriyiyum iyital
233.	āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭu niliyiṭaṉ uṭittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ ā eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉ oṟṟu akaramoṭum niliiṭaṉ uṭittu - muṉpu peṟṟuniṉṟa āṉ eṉṉum colliṉatu ṉakaravoṟṟu akarattoṭum niṟkum iṭaṉuṭittu e-ṟu.
'iṭaṉuṭittu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇattu im muṭipeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi' eṉa varum.
'akaramoṭum' eṉṟa ummiyāṉ, akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmi eṉak koḷka.	(30)
appeyarmuṉṉar īkārapakaram puṇarumāṟu
234.	āṉmuṉ varūum īkārap pakaram
	tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalu murittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum āṉ eṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varum īkārap pakaram - āṉ eṉṉum colmuṉ ṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalum urittu - ap pakaramākiya tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi av vīkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki muṭitaliyum uṭittu e-ṟu.
'tōṉṟi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻip pēṟākiya ṉakara voṟṟiṉ kēṭu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	āppi eṉa varum.
ummiyāṉ, āṉpī eṉpatē perumpāṉmiyeṉak koḷka.	(31)
ākārīṟṟuc cila coṟkaḷ ceyyuṭkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
235.	kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭa ukaram
	aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉi keṭa ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu ciṉi yākiya akāram māttiri keṭa āṇṭu ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyu ḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	"iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa piṇarppaṭu taṭavumutal" (naṟ. 19) eṉa varum.
'aṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukarampeṟātu ciṉikeṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aravaṇi koṭi eṉa varum.
'piṇavunāy muṭukkiya' (malipaṭu. 176)eṉṟāṟpōla varum alvaḻimuṭipu 'kiḷanta valla' (kuṟṟiya. puṇar. 77) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭiya teṉak koḷka.	(32)
ikara īṟṟuppeyarkku vēṟṟumimuṭipu
236.	ikara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu toki marapiṉuṭ kūṟi (159) niṉṟamiyiṉ ataṉ vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - ikaravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar (204) atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutal moḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kiḷikkāl, ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(33)
ivvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷ cilavum viṉiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu
237.	iṉiaṇi eṉṉuṅ kāliyum iṭaṉum
	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyuñ cuṭṭum aṉṉa.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuḷ cila iṭiccollum viṉiccollum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉi aṇi eṉṉum kāliyum iṭaṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭum aṉṉa - iṉi eṉṟum aṇi eṉṟum collappaṭu kiṉṟa kālattiyum iṭattiyum uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccollum, iv vikaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭākiya iṭiccollum, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum taṉmiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iṉikkoṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; ikkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.	(34)
‘iṉṟi’ ceyyuṭkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
238.	iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉiyeñ ciṟuti
	niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal
	toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccak kuṟippiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal - iṉṟi eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyeccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramāyt tirintu muṭital, toṉṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟiyuṭiya ceyyuḷkaḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	"uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā koṟki yōṉē" eṉa varum.
'niṉṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṉ peṟṟuniṉṟa valleḻuttu vīḻkka. 'toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅku' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aṉṟi eṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ im muṭipiṟṟātal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭi taṭpa' (puṟam. 124) eṉa varum.	(35)
ikaraccuṭṭu iyalpukaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
239.	cuṭṭi ṉiyaṟki muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar iyalpukaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki muṉkiḷanta aṟṟu - ikara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉatu iyalpu iyalpukkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum muṉ akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṟkuc collappaṭṭa taṉmittām e-ṟu.
eṉṟatu meṉkaṇam varumvaḻi am melleḻuttu mikkum (uyir. 3), iṭikkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum nilimoḻi vakaram peṟṟum (uyir.  4,5), ceyyuṭ kaṇ vakaramkeṭṭuc cuṭṭunīṇṭum (uyir. 6) muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
e-ṭu:	iññāṉam, innūl, immaṇi eṉavum; ivyāḻ, ivvaṭṭu eṉavum; ivvaṭi, ivvāṭi, iv vuviyam eṉavum; īvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(36)
‘tūṇi’ patakkoṭu puṇartal
240.	patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
	mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumi yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷil aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku mēl tokimarapiṉuḷ (22) eytiya ē eṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) patakku muṉ variṉ tūṇikkiḷavi mutal kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumi iyaṟṟu - patakku eṉṉum col taṉmuṉ variṉ tūṇi eṉṉum col muṉpu (236) vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tūṇippatakku eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, irutūṇippatakku eṉa aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum ivviti koḷka.
'kiḷanteṭutta' eṉṟataṉāṉ, tūṇimuṉṉarp piṟa poruṭ peyar vantavaḻiyum, āṇṭu nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum, taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. iṉṉum, ataṉāṉē, taṉmuṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻi ikkuc cāriyippēṟum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tūṇikkoḷ, tūṇiccāmi eṉavum; irutūṇikkoḷ eṉavum; tūṇittūṇi eṉavum; tūṇikkut tūṇi eṉavum varum.	(37)
‘nāḻi’ uriyoṭu puṇartal
241.	urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi
	iṟuti ikara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	ṭakāra moṟṟum āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uri varu kāli nāḻikkiḷavi iṟuti ikaram mey yoṭum keṭum - uri eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy vanta kālattu nāḻi eṉṉum col taṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa ikaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa mey yoṭum keṭum, a vayiṉ ṭakāram oṟṟum - avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṭuri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu 'iru nāṭuri' eṉṟa vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka.
'ikaram' eṉṉātu 'iṟuti ikaram' eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭi nili moḻiyum varumoḻiyum nilimoḻikaḷāy niṉṟu piṟa poruṭ peyaroṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nāḻikkāyam, urikkāyam eṉa varum.	(38)
‘paṉi’ eṉṉum kālappeyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
242.	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumik(ku)
	attum iṉṉum cāriyi ākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyi peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉi eṉa varum kāla vēṟṟumikku attum iṉṉum cāriyi ākum - paṉi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa paṉikkālatti uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumi muṭipuṭiya peyarkku varum cāriyi attum iṉṉum ākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
'vēṟṟumi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻutti vīḻkka.	(39)
‘vaḷi’ eṉṉum pūtappeyarum aṟṟuātal
243.	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
	avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḷi eṉavarum pūtak kiḷaviyum a iyal niliyal cevvitu eṉpa - vaḷi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟi uṇara niṉṟa collum mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum av viyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
'cevvitu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(40)
‘uti’ melleḻuttu mikutal
244.	utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - uti eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa col valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	utiṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(41)
‘puḷi’ marappeyar ammup peṟutal
245.	puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷimarak kiḷavikku cāriyi am - puḷi eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa colliṟku varum cāriyi ammuc cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟṟiṭi eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ammup peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu (236) vīḻkka.	(42)
cuvip ‘puḷi’ melleḻuttu mikutal
246.	ēṉip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta puḷippeyarkku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikum - am marap peyaraṉṟi oḻinta cuvippuḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiṅkūḻ, cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.	(43)
appeyar valleḻuttum mikappeṟutal
247.	valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉam illi
	olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku valleḻuttu mikumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - ēṉip puḷippeyarmuṉ eytiya melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi. olvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkattāṉa - poruntum iṭamaṟika vaḻakkiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷikkūḻ, cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.
'olvaḻi yaṟital' eṉṟataṉāṉ, puḷiccōṟu eṉṟatupōla maṟṟiyavi vaḻakkup payiṟci ilaveṉpatum koḷka.
'vaḻakkat tāṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttum ilēcum illātaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumiyellām koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kūtāḷaṅkōṭu eṉavum, kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉavum, tuḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, paruttikkuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉavum, kappi tanti kappinti eṉavum, kaṭṭi akal kaṭṭakal eṉavum, kuḷikuṟumi kuḷikkuṟumi eṉavum, iṉṉiṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, 'puḷiṅkāy'  (aiṅkuṟu. 51) eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, iv vīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻtalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa varum.	(44)
ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyar (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) āṉcāriyi peṟutal
248.	nāḷmuṉ tōṉṟun toḻilnilik kiḷavikku
	āṉiṭi varutal aiyam iṉṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyi vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟum toḻilnilik kiḷavikku - ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum viṉic colliṟku, āṉiṭi varutal aiyam iṉṟu - āṉcāriyi iṭi vantu muṭital aiyam illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
ñīaiya miṉṟuñī eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyipu valleḻuttu (236) vīḻkka.(45)
ivvīṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyar (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) ikkup peṟutal
249.	tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyi.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷmuṉ variṉ cāriyi ikku - tiṅkaḷi uṇara niṉṟa ikaravīṟṟup peyarmuṉṉart toḻilnilik kiḷavi variṉ varum cāriyi ikkuccāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(46)
īkārīṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
250.	īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, īkāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻiyiṉkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - īkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu (222) alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu varumvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tīkkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.	(47)
iyalpāy muṭiyum ivvīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cila
251.	nīeṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum
	mīeṉa marīiya iṭamvari kiḷaviyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum mī eṉa marīiya iṭamvari kiḷaviyum - nī eṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarp peyarākiya (pī eṉṉum) īkāravīṟṟup peyarum mī eṉṟu colla varūum maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa, ōr iṭattiṉi varintuṇarttum collum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - mēl iv vīṟṟuṭ kūṟiya valleḻut tup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nī kuṟiyi, ciṟiyi, tīyi, periyi eṉavum;  pī kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; mīkaṇ, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
'nī kuṟiyi' eṉpatu mēl "aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē" (toki. 13) eṉṟavaḻi aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ, mēl (254) vēṟṟumikkaṇ niṉki eṉat tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉka.
mīkaṇ eṉpatu alvaḻi muṭipaṉṟeṉiṉum iyalpātal nōkki uṭaṉkūṟappaṭṭatu.	(48)
avaṟṟuḷ ṉamīṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum uṇmi
252.	iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
	uṭaṉili moḻiyum uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ mī eṉpataṟku vēṟoru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum uṭaṉ nilimoḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - iṭattiṉi varintuṇarttum mī eṉṉum colmuṉ iyalpāy muṭitalēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum tammil iyintu niṟṟaliyuṭiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mīkkōḷ, mīppal eṉa varum.
'uṭaṉili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl eṉa mel leḻuttup peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 	(49)
īkārīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
253.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - īkāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra vīṟṟu (222) al vaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īkkāl, ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	 (50)
‘nī’ niṉ ātal
254.	nīeṉ orupeyar urupiyal niliyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar urupu iyal niliyum - nī eṉa niṉṟa orupeyar urupupuṇarccikkaṇ (180) neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - avvāṟu muṭintaviṭattu iyipu valleḻuttu mikātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	niṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(51)
ukarīṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
255.	ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ukara īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ aka rīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.	(52)
ukaraccuṭṭu (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) valleḻuttu mikku muṭital
256.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattoṭu kūṭi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum a toḻiṟṟu ākum - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varumvaḻi av akaravīṟṟu al vaḻiyiṉ toḻiṟṟāy (205) valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ukkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum.	(53)
atu piṟakaṇam varuvaḻi akaraccuṭṭup pōla muṭiyumāṟu
257.	ēṉavi variṉē mēlnili yiyalpē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavi variṉ mēl nili iyalpu - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉ oḻinta kaṇam varumoḻiyāka variṉ mēl akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭu muṭintu niṉṟa nilimiyiṉ (206, 207, 208, 209) iyalpi yuṭiyatāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi eṉavum; uvyāḻ, uv vaṭṭu eṉavum; uvvaṭi, uvvāṭi, uvvuviyam eṉavum; ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(54)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram iyalpātal
258.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti iyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti iyalpākum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuppeyar mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atukuṟitu, itukuṟitu, utukuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	 (55)
‘aṉṟu’ varuvaḻiyum ‘ai’ varuvaḻiyum atu muṭiyumāṟu
259.	aṉṟuvaru kāli āvā kutalum
	aivaru kāli meyvarintu keṭutalum
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarkku oru ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṟu varukāli ā ākutalum - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukaravīṟṟup peyar aṉṟu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu av vukaram ākāramākit tirintu muṭitalum, ai varukāli mey varintu keṭutalum - ai eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyuṅ kālattu av vukaram tāṉ ūrnta meyyi oḻittuk keṭṭu muṭitalum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ urittu eṉa moḻipa - av viru muṭipum ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma eṉavum; atimaṟṟamma, itimaṟṟamma, utimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.	(56)
ukarīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
260.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ukaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum av vakara vīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	(57)
eru, ceru - eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
261.	eruvuñ ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇit
	tiripiṭaṉ uṭiya teriyuṅ kāli
	ammiṉ makarañ ceruvayiṉ keṭumē
	tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vitiyum, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyi vitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi tiripu iṭaṉ uṭiya teriyum kāli - eru eṉṉum collum ceru eṉṉum collum ammuccāriyiyoṭu porunti muṉ coṉṉa vēṟṟumip potu vitiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭiya ārāyuṅ kālattu; ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṉ keṭum - av vammuc cāriyiyatu īṟṟiṉ makaram ceru eṉṉum colliṭattuk keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟki tam oṟṟu mikūum - avvāṟu keṭṭaviṭattuc ceru eṉpatu valleḻuttākiya iyalpiyuṭiya taṉatu oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eruvaṅkuḻi, cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉavum; ceruvakkaḷam, cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi eṉavum varum.
'teriyuṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, eru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi melleḻuttuppēṟum, valleḻuttuppēṟum, ceru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttuppēṟum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	eruṅkuḻi, erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam eṉa varum.
'valleḻut tiyaṟki' eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi av virumoḻikkum atikāra valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	eruviṉ kaṭumi, ceruviṉ kaṭumi eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, "ammoṭu civaṇit tiripiṭa ṉuṭiya" eṉa valleḻuttiṉkaṇṇatāka varintu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ammuc cāriyi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ peṟuvaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu: eruva ñāṟci, ceruva ñāṟci eṉa varum. 	(58)
ceyyuṭkaṇ ḻakara ukara īṟṟuppeyar muṭiyumāṟu
262.	ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭittē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷuḷ eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakara ukaram nīṭu iṭaṉ uṭittu - iv vīṟṟu moḻi kaḷuḷ ḻakarattoṭu kūṭiya ukaravīṟṟu moḻi av vukaram ūkāramāy nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ uṭittu; a vayiṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭika e-ṟu.
'iṭaṉuṭittu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, itu ceyyuḷiṭatteṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	"paḻūuppal laṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi" (kuṟun. 180) eṉa varum.	(59)
oṭumarappeyar (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) muṭiyumāṟu
263.	oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṭumarak kiḷavi utimara iyaṟṟu - oṭu eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col uti eṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy (244) melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṭuṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(60)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram aṉcāriyi peṟutal
264.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum
	oṟṟiṭi mikāa valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupu puṇarcciyiṟ coṉṉa iyalpilē (177) niṉṟu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu ukaram keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟki oṟṟu iṭimikā - avviṭattu valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
'oṟṟu iṭi mikā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyi vakuppa valleḻuttu vīḻāteṉpatu peṟṟām.
'valleḻut tiyaṟki' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	kaṭuviṉkuṟi, oṭuviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.	(61)
ūkāra īṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
265.	ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāravīṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu (222) alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: koṇmūkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(62)
ivvīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭiyumāṟu
266.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉili moḻikkum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli avvaki variyār.
iḵtu, * iv vīṟṟu viṉiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉili moḻikkum - ūkāravīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya colliṟkum, muṉṉili moḻi yākiya viṉiccolliṟkum, niṉiyumkāli avvaki variyār - ārāyuṅkālattu av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum kūṟṟiṉi nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; kitūk koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
'niṉiyuṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka.	(63)
*(pāṭam) 'iv vīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal' eṉpatu.
ivvīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
267.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ūkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccik kaṇṇum av vākāra vīṟṟu (222) al vaḻiyōṭu otta iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	koṇmūkkuḻām, celavu, tōṟṟam, paṟivu eṉa varum.		(64)
nilimoḻikkaṇ ukareḻuttuppēṟu tōṉṟumiṭam
268.	kuṟṟeḻut timparum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum ukarak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟṟu moḻikkum ōreḻut torumoḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟṟu moḻikkum ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟali vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṭūukkuṟi, tūukkuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
'niṟṟal' eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇippeyar vēṟṟumik kaṇ valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	āṭūukki, makaṭūukki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(65)
‘pū’ (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) muṭiyumāṟu
269.	pūeṉ orupeyar āyiyal piṉṟē
	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakkip perumpāṉmi melleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pū eṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu - pū eṉṉum ūkāra vīṟṟiyuṭiya oru peyar mēṟcoṉṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum anta iyalpillāmiyi uṭittu; eṉavē, vēṟu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - avviṭattu ām melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
melleḻuttup peṟumeṉṟatu, uriyiṟ kōṭalāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
e-ṭu:	pūṅkoṭi, pūkkoṭi; ceyki, tāmam, pantu eṉa varum.	(66)
ū eṉṉum peyar ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭital
270.	ūeṉ orupeyar āvoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, iṉṉum av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki nilimoḻi ṉakaram peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ū eṉ oru peyar āvoṭu civaṇum - ū eṉṟu collappaṭum ūkāravīṟṟi yuṭiya oru peyar ākāra vīṟṟiṉ ā eṉṉum colloṭu porunti, ukaramum (268) valleḻuttum (265) peṟātu nilimoḻi ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu (232) muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ūṉkuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(67)
appeyar akkuccāriyiyum peṟutal
271.	akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittē
	takkavaḻi aṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iṉṉum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akku eṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittu - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ū eṉṉum peyar mēṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akku eṉṉum cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu; vaḻakkattāṉ  takkavaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṭattu am muṭipu takka iṭam aṟika e-ṟu.
'takkavaḻi yaṟital' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi nilimoḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum, muṉ (270) māṭṭēṟṟāṉ vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttuk keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ūṉakkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
'vaḻakkat tāṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	koṇmūviṉ kuḻām eṉa varum.	(68)
āṭūu, makaṭūu - ivvirupeyarum iṉcāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭital
272.	āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum
	iṉṉiṭi variṉum māṉa milli.
iḵtu av vīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkku muṉ eytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi, cāriyiyum peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṭūu makaṭūu a iru peyarkkum - āṭūu makaṭūu vākiya avviraṇṭu peyarkkum, iṉ iṭivariṉum māṉam illi - mēl eytiya ("kuṟṟeḻut timparum' uyir. 65) eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ 'niṟṟal' eṉpataṉāṉ vanta valleḻuttēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭivari ṉum kuṟṟam illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
'māṉa milli' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi mēl ilēciṉā ṉeytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(69)
ekaramum okaramum muṉṉili ēvalorumikkaṇ īṟātal
273.	ekara okaram peyarkkī ṟākā
	muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar
	tēṟṟamuñ ciṟappum alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, ekaravīṟṟiṟkum okaravīṟṟiṟkum īṟākāta niliyil vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekaram okaram peyarkku īṟu ākā - ekaramum okaramum peyarccoṟku īṟu ākā, muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - viṉiccolluḷ muṉṉili moḻiyiṭattaṉaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar, tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻiyāṉa - tēṟṟap poruṇmiyiṉ varum iṭiccol ekāra vīṟum ciṟappup poruṇmiyiṉ varum iṭiccol okāra vīṟum allātaviṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēe eṉavum, ōo eṉavum varum. ivi muṉṉili viṉi. ēe koṇṭāṉ, ōo koṇṭāṉ. ivi iṭiccol.	(70)
mēli ekara okaram pōlātu, tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ okaramum (vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi) iyalpātal
274.	tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā.
itu, muṉ īṟām eṉṉappaṭṭa ekara okara īṟṟu iṭiccoṟkum, av vīṟṟu muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum - tēṟṟap poruṇ miyil varum ekaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum ciṟappup poruṇmiyil varum okaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, mēl kūṟu iyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā - mēli muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkuk kūṟappaṭum iyalpuṭiya valleḻuttu mikāvāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
'mēṟkūṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, 'vantatu (muṉṉili viṉi) koṇṭu vārātatu uṇarka' eṉṉum tantiravutti vakiyāṉ valleḻuttu mikumeṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, nīyēe koṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; (ōokoṇṭēṉ, ōo koṇ ṭāy,) ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum. ivi iṭiccol. ēekkoṟṟā, ōokkoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa ivi muṉṉiliviṉi.
'iyaṟki' eṉṟataṉāṉ, am muṉṉili viṉikaḷi aḷapeṭiyāka niṟīik koḷka.	(71)
ēkārīṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
275.	ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ēkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟu - ēkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ūkāravīṟṟu (265) alvaḻi iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēkkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(72)
ivvīṟṟu iṭiccol muṭiyumāṟu
276.	*māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum
	kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum - māṟupāṭu kōṭaliyuṭiya eccap poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic collum, viṉāpporuṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic collum, eṇṇup poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic collum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉē koṇṭēṉ, ceṉṟēṉ, tantēṉ, pōyiṉēṉ eṉavum; nīyē koṇṭāy, ceṉṟāy, tantāy, pōyiṉāy eṉavum; koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē tēvaṉē pūtaṉē eṉavum varum.
'kūṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinilip poruṇmikkaṇṇum, īṟṟacikkaṇṇum varum ēkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpu muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu pirinili. 'kaṭalē pā ṭeḻun tolikkum' (akam. 350) eṉpatu īṟṟaci.	(73)
*(pāṭam) 'māṟukōḷ eccamum' eṉpatu.
ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
277.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ēkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum av vūkāra vīṟṟu (265) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: 	vēkkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.	(74)
ē eṉum peyar iṟuti ekara eḻuttuppēṟu kōṭal
278.	ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varumē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ē eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum - av vēṟṟumikkaṇ 
ē eṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēekkoṭṭil, cāli, tuḷi, puḻi eṉa varum.
'uriyiṟkōṭal' eṉpataṉāṉ, av vekarappēṟu poruntiṉa vaḻik koḷka.	(75)
cē eṉṉum marappeyaratu muṭipu
279.	cēeṉ marappeyar oṭumara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki mel leḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cē eṉ marappeyar oṭu mara iyaṟṟu - cē eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar oṭumarattiṉ (263) iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(76)
‘cē’ peṟṟamāyiṉ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
280.	peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟiṉ vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta cē eṉpataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum - mēṟkūṟiya cē eṉpatu peṟṟattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa poḻutāyiṉ muṭiya iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiya vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ic cē eṉpatu eṭuttōttāṉ iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum, atuvē marappeyarāy urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi (iṉ) peṟṟavaḻiyum, piṟacol avvāṟu iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu: 	cēviṉkōṭu, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; cēviṉ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum;  ēviṉ kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.	(77)
aikārīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
281.	aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, aikārīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu tokimarapiṉuṭ (e. 159) kūṟi niṉṟamiyiṉ, ataṉ vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - aikāra īṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉikkōṭu, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(78)
cuṭṭumutal aikārīṟṟuppeyar muṭiyumāṟu
282.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutaṟpeyarkku valleḻuttoṭu vaṟṟu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuppeyar urupu puṇarcciyiṟ (178) kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aikāram keṭātum keṭṭum vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟukkōṭu, iviyaṟṟukkōṭu, uviyaṟṟuk kōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; avaṟṟuk kōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟukkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.	(79)
vici, ñemi, nami - eṉṉum marappeyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
283.	vicimarak kiḷaviyum ñemiyum namiyum
	avimup peyaruñ cēmara iyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marappeyar cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vicimarakkiḷaviyum ñemiyum namiyum avi muppeyarum - vici eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum ñemi eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum nami eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum ākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu cē eṉṉum marattiṉatu iyalpiṉavāy (279) melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viciṅkōṭu, ñemiṅkōṭu, namiṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(80)
paṉi, ari, āviri - eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
284.	paṉiyum ariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli ammoṭu civaṇum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti yarivarintu keṭumē
	meyavaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyum ariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum - paṉi eṉṉum collum ari eṉṉum collum āviri eṉṉum collum, niṉiyuṅkāli ammoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅkālattu mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu, am eṉṉum cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum, ai eṉ iṟuti ari varintu keṭum - avviṭattu ai eṉṉum īṟu ari eṉṉum colli nīkkik (ēṉiyiraṇṭilum) keṭum, mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - taṉṉāṉūrappaṭṭa mey ac colliṭattē oḻiyaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉaṅkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; ariyaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āviraṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum varum.
'niṉiyuṅ kāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ piṟavum tūtuṇi, vaḻutuṇi, tilli, ōli eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum ammuk koṭuttu aikāram keṭuttut tūtuṇaṅkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy, tillaṅkāy, ōlampōḻ eṉṟu muṭikka.	(81)
paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar ‘aṭṭu’p puṇarumāṟu
285.	paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli
	niliyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉ uyirē
	ākāram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu varukāli - mēṟkūṟiya vaḻiyēyaṉṟip paṉi eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nili iṉṟākum ai eṉ uyir - niṟṟal iṉṟām ai eṉṉum uyir; a vayiṉāṉa ākāram varutal - avviṭattu ākāram vantu am meymēl ēṟi muṭika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum.
'āvayiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟatu vēṟṟumi muṭipu koḷka.	(82)
paṉiyiṉ muṉṉark ṉakoṭiṉa puṇarumāṟu
286.	koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇ niṟpak
	kaṭinili yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) koṭimuṉ variṉ - koṭi eṉṉum col paṉi eṉṉum colmuṉṉar variṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭinili iṉṟu – *mēṟ (284) keṭukka eṉappaṭṭa aikāram āṇṭuk keṭātē niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti nīkkum nilimi yiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉikkoṭi eṉa varum.
'kaṭinili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēciṉāṉum ammuccāriyiyum piṟa cāriyiyum peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkuc ceṉṟavaḻiyum iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyiṉ kāy, ariyiṉkōṭu, āviriyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; viciyiṉkōṭu, ñemiyiṉ kōṭu, nami yiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; tūtuṇiyiṉkāy, vaḻutuṇiyiṉkāy eṉavum; vaḻiyiṉ kōṭu, vaḻiyiṉ pū eṉavum varum.
'avaṇ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṉittiraḷ eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum paṉintiraḷ eṉṟa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka.	(83)
*(pāṭam) 'mēl keṭumeṉappaṭṭa' eṉpatu.
ivvīṟṟu tiṅkaṭpeyarum nāṭpeyarum muṭiyumāṟu
287.	tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntukiḷan taṉṉa.
iḵtu, iyipu valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyippēṟum, val leḻuttu vilakkic cāriyippēṟum kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu vitiyum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu vitiyum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntu kiḷantaṉṉa - aikāra vīṟṟut tiṅkaḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum av vīṟṟu nāḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyarum muṉ ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaḷum (249) nāḷum (248) kiḷanta taṉmiyavāy ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ; kēṭṭiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nāḷ muṉkūṟātu tiṅkaḷ muṉkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, kariyavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉavum; aviyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; vaḻiṅkōṭu, vaḻik kōṭu eṉavum; kaliṅkōṭu, kalikkōṭu eṉavum ivvīṟṟu muṭiyā taṉavellām koḷka.	(84)
‘maḻi’ muṭiyumāṟu
288.	maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum.
iḵtu, iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attuppēṟum, valleḻuttu vilakki iṉṉum vakukkiṉṟamiyiṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḻi eṉ kiḷavi vaḷi iyal niliyum - maḻi eṉṉum aikāravīṟṟuccol, ikaravīṟṟu vaḷi eṉṉum col (243) attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maḻiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, maḻiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(85)
‘vēṭki’ muṉṉar avā muṭiyumāṟu
289.	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti avāmuṉ variṉē
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar
	ṭakāram ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum ai eṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷiṭattu alvaḻikkaṇ vēṭki eṉṉum aikāra vīṟṟuccol, avā muṉvariṉ - avā eṉṉum col taṉakku muṉvariṉ, meyyoṭum keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - av vikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭuṅkūṭak keṭukaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar; ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāravoṟṟu ṇakāra voṟṟāyt tirintu muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	"vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā" (naṟ. 61) eṉa varum.
itaṉi ummittokiyākak koḷka. avāveṉpatu av vēṭkiyiṉ mikuti.
iv valvaḻiyi vēṟṟumi muṭipiṟku muṉ kūṟātataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōla varum ummittoki alvaḻi muṭipum, pāṟaṅkal eṉa irupeyaroṭṭu alvaḻi muṭipum koḷka.	(86)
ōkāra iṟutip peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
290.	ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ōkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu - *ōkāravīṟṟu alvaḻip peyarccol ēkāravīṟṟu (275) alvaḻi iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōkkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(87)
*(pāṭam) 'ōkāra īṟṟup peyarccol' eṉpatu.
ivvīṟṟu iṭiccol muṭipu
291.	māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum - māṟu pāṭṭiṉik koṇṭa eccapporuṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya ōkāramum, viṉāpporuṇmiyi yuṭiya ōkāramum, aiyapporuṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - muṉ peyarkkuk kūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉōkoṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattō patiṉoṉṟō eṉavum varum.
'kūṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piriniliyum, teriniliyum, ciṟappum, eṇṇum, īṟṟaciyum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṉōkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, naṉṟō tītōvaṉṟu eṉavum, ōo koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, "kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷi ṟeṉkō koyyuḷiya māveṉkō" (puṟam. 387) eṉa vum 'yāṉō tēṟēṉ' (kuṟun. 21) eṉavum varum.	(88)
oḻiyici ōkāra iṭiccol muṭipu
292.	oḻintataṉ niliyum oḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintataṉ niliyum oḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu - oḻiyici ōkārattiṉatu niliyum mēṟcolliyoḻinta ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	koḷalō koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(89)
ivvīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
293.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē
	okaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ōkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum av vōkāravīṟṟu (290) alvaḻiyō ṭottu valleḻuttup peṟṟup puṇarum; a vayiṉāṉa okaram varutal - avviṭattu okaram varuka e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	ōokkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉa varum.	(90)
‘kō’ ‘il’loṭu puṇarumāṟu
294.	illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki ākum - ōkāra vīṟṟuk kō eṉṉum moḻiyiṉi il eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu colliṉ ōkāram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōyil eṉa varum.
nilimoḻi okaraveḻuttuppēṟu variyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumeṉpatu itaṉāṟ peṟṟām.
kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇip peyaraṉṟōveṉiṉ, kōvantatu eṉa aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇippāṟ paṭṭatu pōlum.(91)
oṉcāriyi peṟṟumuṭiyum ivvīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ
295.	urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) urupuiyal niliyum moḻiyumār uḷa - av vīṟṟuṭ cila urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē (181) niṉṟu oṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōoṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
itaṉāṉum peṟṟām, cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakkāmi.	(92)
aukāra īṟṟuppeyar iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
296.	aukāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	alvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
	avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
	cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.
iḵtu, aukāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aukāra iṟuti peyarnili muṉṉar alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikutal varinili iṉṟu - aukāravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ avi alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkkum niliyiṉṟām; a iru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal cevvitu eṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr - av virukūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilimoḻik kaṇ ukaram vantu muṭital cevviteṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuvukkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; kuvuk kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.
'cevvitu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum iṭik kaṇattukkaṇṇum iruvaḻiyum ukarappēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kuvu ñāṉṟatu, kuvuñāṟci eṉavum; kuvu valitu, kuvu valimi eṉavum varum.
'nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuviṉ kaṭumi eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvu koḷka.		(93)

uyirmayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

8
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇat toṭum ciṟupāṉmi piṟa kaṇattoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu.
ñakārīṟu iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumāṟu
297.	ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṟṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ av veḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy iyiyiṉ valleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal - āṇṭu nilimoḻik kaṇ ukaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñuk kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; uriñuk kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.	(1)
avvīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭikkaṇattōṭum muṭiyumāṟu
298.	ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukara niliyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarat toṭum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma va iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum - anta ñakāravīṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ña na ma va mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy iyiyiṉum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; uriñu ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; uriñuvalitu, valimi eṉavum varum.
iṭikkaṇattu yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokimarapiṉuḷ "ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti" (21) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
nakara iṟutiyum ukaraccāriyi peṟṟu muṭital
299.	nakara iṟutiyum ataṉō raṟṟē.
itu, nakaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu oruvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nakara iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - nakarīṟṟup peyarum mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi añ ñakāra īṟṟōṭu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunuk kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; porunu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; porunu valitu eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(3)
avviṟuti vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukkeṭa akaram peṟṟu muṭital
300.	vēṟṟumikku ukkeṭa akara niliyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ nilimoḻi muṭipu vēṟāy muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikku u keṭa akaram niliyum - an nakara vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eytiya nilimoḻi ukaram keṭa akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunak kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum; poruna ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; poruna valimi eṉavum varum.
'akaram niliyum' eṉṉātu 'ukaraṅ keṭa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, av vīr īṟṟiṉ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum ciṟupāṉmi ukarappēṟum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	poruniṉ kuṟi, uri"ṉ kuṟi eṉavum, "uyaval yāṉi verinucceṉ ṟaṉṉa" (akam. 65) eṉavum varum.	(4)
‘verin’ nakaramkeṭa melleḻuttu mikku muṭital
301.	verineṉ iṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
	varumiṭaṉ uṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, an nakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) verin eṉ iṟuti muḻutum keṭuvaḻi - verin eṉṟu *collappaṭum nakaravīṟṟumoḻi taṉ īṟṟu nakaram muṉ peṟṟa akarattōṭu eñcāmaṟ keṭṭaviṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟki varum iṭaṉ uṭittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	veriṅ kuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(5)
*(pāṭam) 'collappaṭṭa' eṉpatu.
āṇṭu valleḻuttum mikappeṟutal
302.	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum am moḻikku eytiyatu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - av verin eṉṉum col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟaviṭattu melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	verik kuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
ñakāravīṟṟoṭu nakāravīṟu otta muṭipiṟṟātaliṉ uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(6)
ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu
303.	ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟu vēṟṟumipporuṭkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - ṇakāra vīṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi iyiyiṉ, ṭakāram ākum vēṟṟumip poruṭku - ṭakāramāy muṭiyum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṭkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.	(7)
āṇ, peṇ - eṉṉum peyarkaḷ (vēṟṟumikkaṇ) iyalpāy muṭital
304.	āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki - āṇ eṉṉum peyarum peṇ eṉṉum peyarum mēl toki marapiṉuḷ 'moḻimuta lākum' (148) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlat tām vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇki, peṇki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
maṟṟitu tokimarapiṉuḷ "aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar" (cūttiram 13) eṉpataṉuḷ iyalpāy muṭintataṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivi āṇṭu muṭintaṉapōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉiyāṟ pālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa vaṉṟi (col. 172), irutiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ av vaḵṟiṇip peyaratu iyalpoṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittār eṉak koḷka. ivvāṟātaliṉ, āṇkaṭitu peṇ kaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻiyum 'moḻimuta lākum' (toki. 5) eṉpataṉuṭ koḷḷappaṭum.	(8)
‘āṇ’ marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟutal
305.	āṇmarak kiḷavi arimara iyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇmarak kiḷavi arimara iyaṟṟu - āṇ eṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarccol arimaram ammuppeṟum iyalpiṟṟāyt (284) tāṉum ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(9)
ceyyuḷuḷ ‘viṇ’ attup peṟutal
306.	viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
	uṇmiyum urittē atteṉ cāriyi
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻilvaru kāli.
itu, ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṇ eṉa varum kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa ākāyatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyi uṇmiyum urittu - attu eṉṉum cāriyi uṇṭātalum urittu illiyātalum urittu, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varu kāli - ceyyuḷiṭattu viṉivarum kālattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viṇṇattuk koṭkum eṉavum, 'viṇkuttu nīḷvari' (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum.	(10)
ṇakārīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iruvaḻiyum ukaram peṟutal
307.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ṇakāra vīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyaratu iyalpāy (297, 298) vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttum ukarappēṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum vantavaḻi ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇukkaṭitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumi eṉavum; maṇṇu ñāṉṟatu, ñāṟci eṉavum; maṇṇuvalitu, valimi eṉavum iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟuvaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	veṇṇukkari, eṇṇuppāṟu, maṇṇuccōṟu eṉa varum.	(11)
ivvīṟṟuk kiḷippeyar iyalpāy muṭital
308.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷippeyar ellām koḷat tiripu ila - ṇakara vīṟṟuḷ ōr iṉatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām tiripuṭiyaveṉṟu karutum paṭiyākat tiritaluṭiyavaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	umaṇkuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruṇmi uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccol tirintu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ūṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; avaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ, uvaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(12)
‘eṇ’ eṉṉum uṇavuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
309.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar
	vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumi muṭipu pōlat tirintu muṭivatu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - vēṟṟumi yallātaviṭattu, eṇ eṉ uṇavuppeyar - eṇ eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa uṇaviṉi yuṇart tum peyar, vēṟṟumi iyaṟki niliyalum urittu - vēṟṟumiyatu tirintu muṭiyum (303) iyalpu niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.
ummiyāṉ, eṇkaṭitu eṉṟu iyalpātalē (148) perum pāṉmi.	(13)
muraṇ eṉum toḻiṟpeyar iyalpāy muṭital
310.	muraṇeṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutaliyal niliyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟpeyar muṭipu vilakki, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumi muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutal iyal niliyum - muraṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum toḻiṟpeyar iv vīṟṟiṟku iruvaḻiyum muṉkūṟiya iyalpum (148), tiripumākiya (303) iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muraṇkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; muraṭ kaṭumi, cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi eṉavum varum.
itaṉit 'toḻiṟpeya rellām' (307) eṉpataṉ piṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumi eṉṉum iyalpum; araṇ kaṭumi, araṭkaṭumi eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyum koḷka.	(14)
makarīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
311.	makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ
	tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, makaravīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya ṇakaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipōṭu iyiya vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ - makaravīṟṟup peyarc col vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - am makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	marakkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
'tuvara' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyartiṇip peyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	marañāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉavum; naṅki, eṅki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; nuṅki, taṅki eṉavum varum.	(15)
ivvīṟṟup peyar akarākāraṅkaḷ varuvaḻi muṭipu vēṟupaṭutal
312.	akara ākāram varūuṅ kāli
	īṟṟumici akaram nīṭalum urittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ākāram varuṅkāli - akara mutalmoḻiyum ākāra mutalmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkāli, īṟṟumici akaram nīṭalum urittu - nilimoḻikkaṇ īṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīḷātu niṟpatēyaṉṟi nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	marāaṭi, kuḷāampal eṉavum; maravaṭi, kuḷavāmpal eṉavum varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuṭ piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kōṇākōṇam, kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉa varum.
muṉṉarc 'celvaḻi yaṟital' (313) eṉpataṉāṉ, kuḷāampal eṉpuḻi varumoḻi ākārakkuṟukkamum, kōṇākōṇam eṉpuḻi varumoḻi valleḻuttukkēṭum koḷka.	(16)
ivvīṟṟup peyar melleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭital
313.	melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyum uḷa - melleḻut tōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; vaḻakkattāṉa celvaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṉkaṇ avi vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuḷaṅkari, kuḷakkari; cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉa varum.
'celvaḻi yaṟital' eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷaṅkari, kuḷakkari eṉṟatu pōla, allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyalla veṉpatu koḷka.
'vaḻakkat tāṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyātaṉa vellām muṭittuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ilavaṅkōṭu eṉavum, 'pulampuk kaṉaṉē' (puṟam. 258) eṉavum, nilattuk kiṭantāṉ eṉavum varum.	(17)
‘illam’ eṉum marappeyar muṭiyumāṟu
314.	illa marappeyar vicimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illa marappeyar vici mara iyaṟṟu - illam eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar viciyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy (283) melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	illaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
itaṉkaṇ makarakkēṭu muṉṉar 'ellām' (315) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	(18)
ivvīṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
315.	alvaḻi yellām melleḻut tākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām melleḻuttu ākum - makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maraṅkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipiṉ muṭiyātaṉa vellām koḷka.
e-ṭu:	marañāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; vaṭṭat taḻi, vaṭṭappalaki eṉavum; kalakkoḷ, kalanel eṉavum; nīlakkaṇ, pavaḷavāy eṉavum; nilanīr eṉavum; kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, paṟakkunāri eṉavum varum.	(19)
‘akam’ ki eṉum varumoḻiyoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
316.	akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉē
	mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
	varinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
	melleḻuttu mikutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akam eṉ kiḷavikku ki muṉvariṉ - akam eṉṉum colliṟku ki eṉṉum col muṉvariṉ, mutal nili oḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum - muṉ 'makaraviṟuti' (311) eṉṟataṉāṉ makaram keṭṭu niṉṟa nilimoḻi mutal niṉṟa akaram oḻiya ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa akaramum akarattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa kakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭitalum avi keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum, varinili iṉṟu āciriyarkku - nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku; a vayiṉṉāṉa melleḻuttu mikutal - av viraṇṭiṭattum melleḻuttu mikku muṭika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṅki, akaṅki eṉa varum.	(20)
‘ilam’ paṭu eṉum varumoḻiyoṭu muṭital
317.	ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli
	niliyalum urittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu uriccol oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ilam eṉ kiḷavikkup paṭu varukāli - ilam eṉṉum colliṟkup paṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, niliyalum urittuc ceyyuḷāṉa - muṉ 'makaraviṟuti' (311) eṉpataṉāṟ keṭṭa īṟu keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittuc ceyyuṭkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	"ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟaki niṟiya" (malipaṭu. 576) eṉa varum.
