(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vakāram varu vaḻi - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉ kaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara varumoḻi varum iṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakārattu urupu ākum - varumoḻiyāya vakarattu uruvāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.
'tōṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, mutal nīḷātu oṟṟiṉṟi muvaṭṭi eṉṟumām.		(46)
nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu vakaram varuvaḻi lakaramātal
453. 	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu lakāram ākum - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu vakaram vantāl lakāra oṟṟāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum. 	(47)
vakaram varuvaḻi, aintaṉ nakaravoṟṟuk keṭutal
454.	ainta ṉoṟṟē muntiyatu keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu muntiyatu keṭum - aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu vakaram vantāl muṉ niṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.
'munti' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nakara oṟṟukkeṭātu av vakaramāyt tirintu aivvaṭṭi eṉṟum ām. 	(48)
oṉṟu iraṇṭu uyirkkaṇam varuvaḻi, ōr īr ātal
455. 	mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ uyirvaru kālit
	tavaleṉa moḻipa ukarak kiḷavi
	mutaṉili nīṭal āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mutal īr eṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varu kāli - muṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭu eṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir mutalmoḻi varuṅkālattu, ukarak kiḷavi taval eṉa moḻipa - ukaramākiya eḻuttuk keṭum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar, mutalnili a vayiṉ nīṭal - av veṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ avviṭattu nīṇṭu muṭiyum. e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrakal, īrakal; ōruḻakku, īruḻakku eṉa varum. 	(49)
uyirvaruvaḻi mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu eṉpaṉa vakaram
varuvaḻip pōl tirintu muṭital
456. 	mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum
	tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟki yākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintu eṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇum aintu eṉṉum eṇṇuc collum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkiyākum - (mēl) tōṉṟi muṭinta vakarattu iyaṟkiyāy mūṉṟaṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum nāṉkaṉkaṇ (eḻut. 453, 454, 455)lakara oṟṟāyum aintaṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvakal, muvvuḻakku eṉavum; nālakal, nāluḻakku eṉavum; aiyakal, aiyuḻakku eṉavum varum.
'tōṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl (457) mūṉṟu eṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭuttuk koḷka. 	(50)
‘uḻakku’ varuvaḻi, potu vitiyāṉ kuṟukiniṉṟa
‘mūṉṟu’ nīṇṭu muṭitalum uṇmi
457. 	mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalum urittē
	uḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ mutalnili uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ mutalil niṉṟa eḻuttu uḻakku eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyatu vaḻakkiṭattu, nīṭalum urittu - kuṟukātu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
'vaḻakkattāṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, akal eṉpataṉkaṇṇum ic ceyki koḷka.
e-ṭu:	mūvakal eṉa varum.	(51)
avvaḻi, kuṟukiniṉṟa ṉāṟuṉa nīṇṭu muṭital
458. 	āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutalnī ṭummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟu eṉ kiḷavi mutal nīṭum - āṟu eṉṉum col (uyir mutalmoḻi vantāl muṉ kuṟuki niṉṟa) mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṟakal, āṟuḻakku eṉa varum. 	(52)
‘oṉpatu’ nāṟkaṇamum varuvaḻi iṉpeṟṟu muṭital
459. 	oṉpā ṉiṟuti uruvunili tiriyātu
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi moḻiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti uruvu nili tiriyātu cāriyi moḻi iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟuti taṉ vaṭivu nili tiriyātu cāriyi moḻiyākiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; oṉpatiṉkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
'uruvu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṉpatiṟṟakal eṉpuḻi iraṭṭiya ṟakaramākak koḷka. 	(53)
‘nūṟu’ varuvaḻi, nilimoḻi oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṟkūṟiya taṉmiyavātal
460.	nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya iyalpē.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟu eṉpataṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu muṉ variṉum kūṟiya iyalpu - nūṟu eṉpatu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaṇ muṉ variṉum (mēl patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarum vaḻi) kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟu, irunūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivi māṭṭēṟṟāṉē muṭintaṉa. 	(54)
avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu nakāram ātal
461.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāram ākum.
itu, māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu nakāram ākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu nakara oṟṟākum.
e-ṭu:	munnūṟu eṉa varum. 	(55)
nāṉkum aintum tam oṟṟut tiriyāmi
462. 	nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā - nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇum aintu eṉṉum eṇṇum tam oṟṟukkaḷ nili tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
'mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṉūṟu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi oṟṟākiya nakarakkēṭu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, oṟṟiṉṟi ainūṟu eṉa varum muṭipum koḷka. 	(56)
oṉpataṉ muṉṉar nūṟu puṇarntu toḷḷāyiram eṉamuṭital
463. 	oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntukiḷan taṟṟē
	munti yoṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum
	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
	ūā vākum iyaṟkit teṉpa
	āyiṭi varutal ikāra rakāram
	īṟumey keṭuttu makāram oṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ mutal nili muntu kiḷantu aṟṟu - oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ mutal niṉṟa okaram mēl patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarum vaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu pōla (445) okaramicit takara oṟṟu mikum, munti oṟṟu ḷakāram iraṭṭum - av vokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟām, nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi nakāra mey keṭa ū ā ākum iyaṟkittu eṉpa - varumoḻiyākiya nūṟu eṉṉum collum nakāra mākiya meykeṭa (ataṉmēl ēṟiya) ūkāram ākāramām iyalpi yuṭittu eṉpar pulavar, a iṭi ikāram rakāram varutal - am moḻiyiṭi ōrikaramum rakāramum varum, īṟu mey keṭuttu makāram oṟṟum - itaṟku īṟākiya kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉiyum aḵtu ēṟi niṉṟa ṟakāra oṟṟiṉiyum keṭuttu oru makaram oṟṟāy vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
'mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa pakaram keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum. 	(57)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉar āyiram puṇarumāṟu - mutal īreṇkaḷ (ukaram keṭa avi) ōr ir eṉa niṟṟal
464. 	āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kāli
	mutalī reṇṇiṉ ukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷmuṉ āyiram eṉpatu varuṅkāl muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirak kiḷavi varum kāli mutal īr eṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum - āyiram eṉṉum col oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varuṅ kālattu mutal īr eṇṇiṉkaṇ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaram keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orāyiram, irāyiram eṉa varum. 	(58)
āṇṭu okara ikaraṅkaḷ nīṭalum uṭimi
465. 	mutaṉili nīṭiṉum māṉa milli.
iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalnili nīṭiṉum māṉam illi - ammutal īr eṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa okara ikaraṅkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrāyiram, īrāyiram eṉa varum. 	(59)
mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vakāramātal
466. 	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāram ākum.
ituvum, māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvā muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vakāram ākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu vakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
'mutaṉili' (465) eṉṟataṉāṉ mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟuk keṭṭu mūvāyiram eṉṟum varum. 	(60)
nāṉkaṇ oṟṟu lakāramātal
467. 	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāram ākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu lakāram ākum - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālāyiram eṉa varum. 	(61)
aintaṉ oṟṟu yakāramātal
468. 	ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāram ākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu yakāram ākum - aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiyāyiram eṉa varum. 	(62)
āṟu ‘aṟu’ eṉak kuṟuki niṟpavē, īṟṟu ukaram keṭṭu muṭital
469. 	āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaram
	īṟumey oḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram īṟu mey oḻiya keṭutal vēṇṭum - āṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟuk keṭātu niṟpa ukaramākiya avvīṟu tāṉē keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
'tirintataṉ tiripatu' eṉṉum nayattāṉ, 'āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ' eṉṟu ōtappaṭṭatu. āṟu eṉpatu aṟu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāka ōtappaṭṭu niṉṟamiyiṉ av vukarakkēṭu ōtappaṭṭatu.
'īṟu' eṉavum 'mey' eṉavum av vuyirmeyyip pirittuc ceyki ōtiṉamiyāṉ, av vuyirmeyyiṉi oṟṟumi nayattāṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu ōtiṉārākak koḷka.
'āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ īṟu mey yoḻiyak keṭum' eṉṉātu 'kuṟṟiyalukaram' eṉṟōtiṉamiyāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātē niṉṟu āṟāyiram eṉṟum ām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē 'āṟā kuvatē' (col. 79) eṉṟāṟpōlap poruṭpeyarkkaṇ varumuṭipum koḷka.
mēl māṭṭēṟṟāṉē (460) eṇṇāyiram eṉa muṭintatu.	(63)
‘oṉpatu’ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭital
470. 	oṉpā ṉiṟuti urupunili tiriyātu
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi marapē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti urupu nili tiriyātu cāriyi marapu iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram taṉ vaṭivu nili tirintu keṭātu cāriyiyākiya marapiṉiyuṭiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
'urupu' eṉṟum, 'nili' eṉṟum, 'cāriyi marapu' eṉṟum kūṟiya mikutiyāṉ, āyiramallāta piṟa eṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭpeya riṭattum, iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṟṟukkōṭi, oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu; oṉpatiṟṟut taṭakki (paripāṭal 3) oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu (toki. 28) eṉa varum.
iṉṉum av vilēcāṉē mēl eṇṇāyiram eṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭuk koḷka. 	(64)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉar nūṟāyiram muṭiyumāṟu
471. 	nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kāli
	nūṟe ṉiyaṟki mutaṉilik kiḷavi.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟu eṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttu āyiram eṉpatu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟāyiram muṉvaru kāli mutalnili kiḷavi nūṟu eṉ iyaṟki - nūṟāyiram eṉṉum aṭiyaṭutta moḻi (oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ) muṉvaruṅ kālattu mutaṉilik kiḷaviyākiya oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇ (mēl oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu muṭinta) nūṟu eṉṉum col av voṉṟaṉoṭu muṭinta vikāra iyaṟki eyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
vaḻinilik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutal eṇkaḷ vikāram eytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟāyiram eṉa varum. irunūṟāyiram, iraṇṭu nūṟāyiram; munnūṟāyiram, mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram; nāṉūṟā yiram, nāṉku nūṟāyiram; ainnūṟāyiram, aintu nūṟāyiram; aṟunūṟāyiram, āṟu nūṟāyiram; eṇṇūṟāyiram, eṭṭu nūṟāyiram; oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, toḷḷāyiram eṉṟa muṭipi ṉoṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka.
'muṉ' eṉpataṉāṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram eṉṟum ām.
'nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāy muṭiyum muṭipiṉkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟukāmi kaṇṭu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē nāṉūṟāyiram eṉpuḻi varumoḻi oṟṟākiya nakarakkēṭu koḷka. 	(65)
nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ puṇarumāṟu
472. 	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ(ku)
	īṟuciṉi yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
itu, nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku īṟu ciṉi oḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikum - nūṟu eṉṉum col oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu īṟākiya kuṟṟiyalukaramum av vukaram ēṟiya meyyākiya ciṉiyum keṭātu niṟpac ciṉikku iṉamākiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟoṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻ, eṭṭu, oṉpatu eṉa varum.
'īṟuciṉi' eṉṟu ōtiya mikiyāṉē nūṟu eṉpataṉoṭu piṟa eṇṇum piṟa poruṭpeyarum iv vitiyum piṟa vitiyum peṟṟu muṭiyumāṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟu eṉavum; nūṟṟukkuṟi, nūṟṟaṭukku eṉavum varum.	(66)
ataṉōṭu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ
aṭiyaṭuttavaḻip puṇarumāṟu
473. 	aviyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, an nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭiyaṭuttavaḻip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avi ūrpattiṉum atoḻiṟṟu ākum - an nūṟu eṉpatu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pattiṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu attoḻiṟṟāy iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟorupaḵtu; irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu, eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum.
'ākum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi yaṭiyaṭuttu varum muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa oṭṭuka. 	(67)
ataṉōṭu, aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu
474. 	aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā
	kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum
	muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, an nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum a iyal tiriyā - (an nūṟu eṉpata ṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum mēṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ tiriyātē niṉṟu iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalu karamum valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyum muṉ kiḷanta aṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar - avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭāmiyum (iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyāy niṉṟamiyāṉ varumoḻi) valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum mēl (vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk) kūṟiya taṉmiyaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟukkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
'tiriyā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟu eṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟṟukkalam, irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa oṭṭuka. 	(68)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭu, 
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ puṇarumāṟu
475. 	oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē
	niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi - oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākap pattu eṉṉum eṇ ūrappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku - av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉṟa āytam keṭutal vēṇṭum - āṇṭu niṉṟa āytam keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum, oṟṟu iṭi mikum - (keṭṭa vaḻi iṉavoṟṟāy oru takara) oṟṟu iṭi mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu, orupattiraṇṭu, irupattiraṇṭu, orupattumūṉṟu, irupattumūṉṟu eṉa oṭṭik koḷka.
'niṉṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl (476)  orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉpēṟum koḷka. 	(69)
av vaṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭu, āyiram vantu muṭiyumāṟu
476. 	āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyi
	āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.
iḵtu, av vaṭiyaṭutta pattiṉōṭu āyirattiṉip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉ cāriyi iṉ ām - (av voṉṟu mutalākiya pattūr kiḷavi muṉ) āyiram eṉpatu variṉ iṭi vantu puṇaruñcāriyi iṉ ām, a vayiṉ oṟṟu iṭi mikutal illi - avviṭattu muṉ kūṟiya oṟṟu iṭi mikutal iṉṟi muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉāyiram, irupatiṉāyiram eṉa oṭṭuka.
'āvayiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku ukaramum valleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka.	(70)
avaṟṟōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantu muṭiyumāṟu
477. 	aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā.
iḵtu, av voṉṟu mutalākiya pattūr kiḷavi muṉ aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum a iyal tiriyā - (avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattūr kiḷavi muṉ) aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum mēl āyirattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi muṭinta iyalpiṉ tiriyātē niṉṟu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉkalam, irupatiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; oru patiṉ kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum koḷka.
'tiriyā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭi ṟakaramākalum, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṉum muṭipiṉ kaṇ varumoḻi nakaram tirintavaḻi nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakarakkēṭum koḷka  (150, 161).	(71)
mutaṉili eṇ oṉṟu eṉpatu, vaṉmi meṉmi iṭikkaṇam varuvaḻi, oru eṉat tirintu muṭital
478. 	mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
	ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyiyiṉum
	mutaṉili yiyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭpeyarip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalnili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum ña na ma tōṉṟiṉum ya va vantu iyiyiṉum - mutaṉili eṇṇākiya oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum ñanamak kaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum ya vak kaḷākiya iṭi yeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu poruntiṉum, mutalnili iyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar - av voṉṟu muṉ eytiya muṭipunili eyti muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
eṉavē, vaḻinili eṇṇākiya iraṇṭumutalākiya eṇkaḷ am (mutaṉili muṭipākiya) muṟkūṟiya vikāram eytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	orukal, cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa varum. irukal, iraṇṭukal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉavum oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka.
oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmiyiṉ oḻikka.
'nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, māṭṭēṟṟukku (171) ēlāta ñakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṭipu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 	(72)
avveṇ uyireḻuttum yāvum varuvaḻi ṉōrṉa eṉat tirital
479. 	ataṉili uyirkkum yāvarum kāliyum
	mutaṉili okaram ōvā kummē
	rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum mēṟkūṟiya yakaram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ataṉ nili uyirkkum yā varukāliyum mutal nili okaram ō ākum - av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattum yā mutal moḻi vanta iṭattum mutalnili eṇṇākiya oṉṟu eṉpataṉkaṇ okaram ōkāram ām, rakarattu ukaram tuvarakeṭum - (avviṭattu) rakarattu ukaram muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
eṉavē, vaḻinili yeṇkaḷuḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṉkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	ōraṭi ōrāṭi eṉavum; iruvaṭi, iruvāṭi iraṇ ṭaṭi, iraṇṭāṭi eṉavum uyir mutal moḻikaḷi oṭṭik koḷka.
yā mutalmoḻi ōr yāḻ eṉa varum.
'tuvara' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum ceyyuḷakattu īraci eṉavum mūvaci eṉavum mutal nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟu koḷka.
'ataṉili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutaṉili nīḷātē niṉṟu ukaram keṭṭu oraṭi, orāṭi, oryāḻ eṉa varum muṭipum koḷka. 	(73)
iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉar ṉamāṉa vantu puṇarumāṟu
480. 	iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
	vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
	makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.
iḵtu, iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉar aḷavumutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya mā eṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇṭu mutalāka oṉpatu īṟākac collappaṭukiṉṟa eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē kiṭakkiṉṟa (vilaṅku maram mutaliya allāta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuriya) mā eṉṉum col tōṉṟiṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu - (iyal pāy muṭitalēyaṉṟi) mēṟkūṟiya (446) maṇṭi eṉṉum aḷavuppeya rōṭu ottu vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉavum peṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iraṇṭumā, irumā, mūṉṟumā, mummā eṉa oṉpatiṉ kāṟum ivvāṟu oṭṭuka.
'iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ' eṉṟu eṭuttamiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumā eṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā eṉṉum muṭipu illiyāyiṟṟu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, mikka eṇṇoṭu kuṟinta eṇ varuṅkāl ummit tokiyākavum, kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikkatu variṟ paṇputtoki yākavum muṭittār eṉka. 	 (74)
lakāra ṉakāra īṟṟuc ceyyuḷmuṭipu
481. 	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
	ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic
	ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, lakāra ṉakāra īṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi iṟutimuṉ - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ, ummum keḻuvum uḷappaṭa piṟavum - um eṉṉum cāriyiyum keḻu eṉṉum cāriyiyum uḷappaṭap piṟa cāriyiyum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic ceyyuḷ toṭar vayiṉ mey peṟa niliyum - ap peṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic ceyyuḷ moḻikaḷit toṭarntu collum iṭattu meymmi peṟa nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷ vayiṉāṉa - vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	'vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum' 'kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē' (kuṟun. 71) eṉavum, 'mānitik kiḻa vaṉum pōṉm' (akam. 66) eṉavum, 'kāṉkeḻu nāṭu' (akam. 97) eṉavum varum.
'moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟi' eṉṟa mikiyāṉ, piṟa īṟṟuḷḷum ic cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'tuṟikeḻu mānti' (naṟ. 35) 'vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar' (akam. 17) eṉa varum.
'aṉṉa marapiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyi kāraṇamāka valleḻuttup peṟutalum, atu kāraṇamāka nilimoḻiyīṟu tiritalum, cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum, ekara nīṭciyum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pūkkēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻ tirunakar eṉṟu avvāṟu vantami yaṟika.
'meypeṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ic cāriyippēṟṟiṉkaṇ īṟṟu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. 	(75)
ivvatikārattup puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ
482. 	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
	kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉun tōṉṟi
	neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
	uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ
	aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum
	ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
	meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
	tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
	eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉavi yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
	puṇariyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā.
iḵtu, iv vatikārattup puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ iviyeṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākik kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉum tōṉṟi neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccol kiḷaviyum - uyireḻuttum puḷḷiyeḻuttum īṟākik kuṟippiṉkaṇṇum paṇpiṉ kaṇṇum iciyiṉkaṇṇum tōṉṟi oru neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟic coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi a iru maruṅ kiṉ aimpāl aṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum - uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yākiya av viraṇṭu tiṇiyiṭattum uḷavāya aintu pāliṉiyum aṟiya varum paṇputtoki moḻikaḷum, ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ mey oruṅku iyalum toḻiltoku moḻiyum -  ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉpaṭi oruṅku naṭakkum viṉic col tokka viṉittokiyum, tam iyal kiḷappiṉ - (eṇkaḷ) tam iyalpu kiḷakkum iṭattu, tam muṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa piṟavum aṉṉavi ellām - (niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy vārātu) tammuṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokutiyum uṭ paṭṭa piṟavum at taṉmiyavellām, maruviṉ pāttiya - vaḻakkiṭattu maruvi naṭanta iṭattuḷḷaṉa (ākalāṉ avvāṟē koḷḷappaṭum). puṇar iyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā - puṇarcci iyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇ (avaṟṟiṉ muṭipu) viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viṇviṇittatu, kārkaṟuttatu, ollolittatu: ivi kuṟiccoṟ kiḷavi āyiṉamiyiṉ muṭikkap paṭāvāyiṉa. viṇṇeṉa vicittateṉa iṭiccolloṭu kūṭiya vaḻip puṇarkkappaṭum.
karuñcāṉṟāṉ eṉṟatu paṇputtoki. itu karum eṉap paṇpuṇara niṉṟatu. karuñcāṉṟāṉeṉat tokiyāyavaḻi karumeṉpatu kariyāṉeṉap pālkāṭṭi niṟṟalil puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu.
kolyāṉi eṉṟatu viṉittoki. atuvum kol leṉat toḻiṉmi uṇaraniṉṟatu. kolyāṉi eṉat toki yāya vaḻi, koṉṟeṉak kālam kāṭṭi niṉṟamiyiṉ puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu.
ōroṉṟu eṉa itu, tammuṟ ṟām vanta eṇ. itu niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum aṉmiyiṟ puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu. itu tāṉē ōroṉṟākak koṭu eṉpuḻip puṇarkkappaṭum.
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyātaṉa uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ eṉpaṉa. ivi uṇ eṉavum karumi eṉavum pirittu niṟuttiyavaḻi āṉum aṉṉum kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yaṉmiyāṉ vantu puṇarāmiyiṉ, puṇarkkappaṭāvāyiṉa.
'tōṉṟi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, koḷḷeṉakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik koḷḷeṉpataṉi eṉa eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka.
'mey oruṅkiyalum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻic ceyki yum kālamum pālum tōṟṟi niṟkumāṟu pirittup puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka. 	(76)
ivvatikārap puṟaṉaṭi
483. 	kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
	viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
	naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, iv vatikārap puṟaṉaṭi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum - muṉ eṭuttōtiṉa allātaṉa ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiyum, vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum - vaḻakku naṭattumiṭattu maruvutaloṭu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiyum, viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa tōṉṟiṉ - muṉ coṉṉa muṭipiyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ, nalmati nāṭṭattu vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal - nallaṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉ vaḻakku iyalumiṭattu avaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷi yaṟintu naṭattuka, eṉmaṉār pulavar - eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	naṟavaṅkaṇṇi, 'kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu' (akam. 97), 'puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉal' (akam. 80), 'poṉṉantikiri', (puṟam. 365) āraṅkaṇṇi, kāṉalam peruntuṟi (aiṅkuṟu. 158) eṉa ivi vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
"vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu naralici" (naṟ. 62) eṉpatu rakaravīṟu attuppeṟṟu muṭintatu. 'muḷavumā', (akam. 182) piṇavunāy eṉpaṉa, alvaḻikkaṇ meṉkaṇattuk kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
'añcevi niṟiya mantiram kūṟi' eṉpatu akam eṉṉum nilimoḻi cevi eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.
'āyiṭi' eṉpatu avveṉṉum vakaravīṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.
taṭavuttiri, taṭavuttōḷ eṉpaṉa uriccol muṭipu.
arumaruntaṉṉāṉ eṉaṟpālatu arumaruntāṉ eṉa marūuvāy muṭintatu. cōṇāṭu malāṭu eṉpaṉavum atu.
potiyi leṉpatum atu. piṟavum aṉṉa. 	(77)

oṉpatāvatu kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

eḻuttatikāram iḷampūraṇaruri muṟṟuppeṟṟatu.
